 20108: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_assignment_punctuator(CToken token)
 20109: {
 20110:     u64 assignments = C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_ASSIGN) | C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_STAR_ASSIGN) |
 20111:                       C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_SLASH_ASSIGN) | C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_PERCENT_ASSIGN) |
 20112:                       C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_PLUS_ASSIGN) | C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_MINUS_ASSIGN) |
 20113:                       C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_SHIFT_LEFT_ASSIGN) | C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_SHIFT_RIGHT_ASSIGN) |
 20114:                       C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_AMPERSAND_ASSIGN) | C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_CARET_ASSIGN) |
 20115:                       C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_PIPE_ASSIGN);
 20116:     bool result = token.kind == C_TOKEN_PUNCTUATOR && (assignments & C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(token.punctuator)) != 0;
 20117:     return result;
 20118: }

 20128:     if (end > start && c_parse_expression_operator_precedence(preprocess.tokens[end - 1]) &&
 20129:         !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[end - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_PLUS_PLUS) &&
 20130:         !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[end - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_MINUS_MINUS))
 20131:     {
 20132:         machine->expression_constraint = S8("could not lower logical expression core");
 20133:         machine->expression_constraint_token = end;
 20134:     }
 20135:     c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, machine->expression_constraint, start, machine->expression_constraint_token);
 20136:     return valid;
 20137: }

 20139: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL u32 c_parse_constraint_expression_start(CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess, u32 start, u32 end,
 20140:                                                            u32 const* openers)
 20141: {
 20142:     u32 cursor = end;
 20143:     bool done = false;
 20144:     while (cursor > start && !done)
 20145:     {
 20146:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[cursor - 1];
 20147:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACKET))
 20148:         {
 20149:             u32 open = openers[cursor - 1 - start];
 20150:             if (open < cursor && open >= start)
 20151:             {
 20152:                 // A control header ends before its unbraced body. In particular,
 20153:                 // if (condition) (*pointer()) = value is a dereferenced place,
 20154:                 // not an assignment to a call result spanning the condition.
 20155:                 bool control = open > start && c_token_in_well_known_set(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[open - 1],
 20156:                     C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(IF) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(WHILE) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(FOR) |
 20157:                     C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(SWITCH));
 20158:                 if (control) done = true;
 20159:                 else cursor = open;
 20160:             }

 20088: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider_kind(CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic, String8 message,
 20089:                                                                  CDiagnosticKind kind, u32 order_token_index, u32 location_token_index)
 20090: {
 20091:     if (message.length && (!diagnostic->message.length || order_token_index < diagnostic->order_token_index))
 20092:     {
 20093:         *diagnostic = (CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic){
 20094:             .message = message,
 20095:             .order_token_index = order_token_index,
 20096:             .location_token_index = location_token_index,
 20097:             .kind = kind,
 20098:         };
 20099:     }

 20108: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_assignment_punctuator(CToken token)
 20109: {
 20110:     u64 assignments = C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_ASSIGN) | C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_STAR_ASSIGN) |
 20111:                       C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_SLASH_ASSIGN) | C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_PERCENT_ASSIGN) |
 20112:                       C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_PLUS_ASSIGN) | C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_MINUS_ASSIGN) |
 20113:                       C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_SHIFT_LEFT_ASSIGN) | C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_SHIFT_RIGHT_ASSIGN) |
 20114:                       C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_AMPERSAND_ASSIGN) | C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_CARET_ASSIGN) |
 20115:                       C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(C_PUNCTUATOR_PIPE_ASSIGN);
 20116:     bool result = token.kind == C_TOKEN_PUNCTUATOR && (assignments & C_PUNCTUATOR_BIT(token.punctuator)) != 0;
 20117:     return result;
 20118: }
 20119: 
 20120: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_checked_expression_type(CTypeParseMachine* machine, Arena* arena, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 20121:                                                           CParseResult* result, CScopeId scope, u32 start, u32 end, CTypeId* type,
 20122:                                                           CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 20123: {
 20124:     machine->validate_expression_constraints = true;
 20125:     machine->expression_constraint = (String8){0};
 20126:     bool valid = c_parse_expression_type_query(machine, arena, preprocess, result, scope, start, end, type);
 20127:     machine->validate_expression_constraints = false;
 20128:     if (end > start && c_parse_expression_operator_precedence(preprocess.tokens[end - 1]) &&
 20129:         !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[end - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_PLUS_PLUS) &&
 20130:         !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[end - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_MINUS_MINUS))
 20131:     {
 20132:         machine->expression_constraint = S8("could not lower logical expression core");
 20133:         machine->expression_constraint_token = end;
 20134:     }
 20135:     c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, machine->expression_constraint, start, machine->expression_constraint_token);
 20136:     return valid;
 20137: }
 20138: 
 20139: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL u32 c_parse_constraint_expression_start(CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess, u32 start, u32 end,
 20140:                                                            u32 const* openers)
 20141: {
 20142:     u32 cursor = end;
 20143:     bool done = false;
 20144:     while (cursor > start && !done)
 20145:     {
 20146:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[cursor - 1];
 20147:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACKET))
 20148:         {
 20149:             u32 open = openers[cursor - 1 - start];
 20150:             if (open < cursor && open >= start)
 20151:             {
 20152:                 // A control header ends before its unbraced body. In particular,
 20153:                 // if (condition) (*pointer()) = value is a dereferenced place,
 20154:                 // not an assignment to a call result spanning the condition.
 20155:                 bool control = open > start && c_token_in_well_known_set(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[open - 1],
 20156:                     C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(IF) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(WHILE) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(FOR) |
 20157:                     C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(SWITCH));
 20158:                 if (control) done = true;
 20159:                 else cursor = open;
 20160:             }
 20161:             else
 20162:             {
 20163:                 done = true;
 20164:             }
 20165:         }
 20166:         else if (token.kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER && c_token_in_well_known_set(preprocess.spelling_base, token,
 20167:                      C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(RETURN) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(CASE) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(GOTO) |
 20168:                      C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(ELSE) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(DO)))
 20169:         {
 20170:             done = true;
 20171:         }
 20172:         else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE) ||
 20173:                  c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACE) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS) ||
 20174:                  c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA) ||
 20175:                  c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_COLON) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_QUESTION) ||
 20176:                  c_parse_assignment_punctuator(token))
 20177:         {
 20178:             done = true;
 20179:         }
 20180:         else
 20181:         {
 20182:             cursor -= 1;
 20183:         }
 20184:     }
 20185:     BUSTER_UNUSED(result);
 20186:     return cursor;
 20187: }
 20188: 
 20189: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL u32 c_parse_constraint_expression_end(CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess, u32 start, u32 end)
 20190: {
@@ 20560:20670 @@
 20560: // `++` there is the operand of an indirection, `*--p = c`, whose
 20561: // destination is the pointee (QuickJS's
 20562: // `js_u64toa`, musl's `vfprintf`, libpng's `png_format_number`). Taking the
 20563: // pointer's type for it converted the character to `char *`.
 20564: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_assignment_identifier_is_operand(CPreprocessResult preprocess, u32 start, u32 index)
 20565: {
 20566:     bool operand = index > start && preprocess.tokens[index - 1].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER;
 20567:     if (operand && index > start + 1)
 20568:     {
 20569:         CToken before = preprocess.tokens[index - 2];
 20570:         operand = !c_token_is_punctuator(&before, C_PUNCTUATOR_DOT) && !c_token_is_punctuator(&before, C_PUNCTUATOR_ARROW) &&
 20571:                   !c_token_is_punctuator(&before, C_PUNCTUATOR_STAR) && !c_token_is_punctuator(&before, C_PUNCTUATOR_AMPERSAND) &&
 20572:                   !c_token_is_punctuator(&before, C_PUNCTUATOR_PLUS_PLUS) && !c_token_is_punctuator(&before, C_PUNCTUATOR_MINUS_MINUS);
 20573:     }
 20574:     return operand;
 20575: }

 20577: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_const_assignments(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 20578:                                                             CDeclaration const* declaration, u8 const* skipped, u32 first_local, u32 const* next_local, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 20579: {
 20580:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 20581:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 20582:     u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 20583:     for (u32 assignment_index = start; assignment_index < end; assignment_index += 1)
 20584:     {
 20585:         CToken assignment_token = preprocess.tokens[assignment_index];
 20586:         if (!c_token_is_punctuator(&assignment_token, C_PUNCTUATOR_ASSIGN) ||
 20587:             !c_parse_assignment_identifier_is_operand(preprocess, start, assignment_index) ||
 20588:             (assignment_index > start + 1 && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[assignment_index - 2], C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS)))
 20589:         {
 20590:             continue;
 20591:         }
 20592:         CScopeId assignment_scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, assignment_index);
 20593:         CEntityId destination_id = c_parse_lookup_entity_token(result, preprocess.spelling_base, assignment_scope,
 20594:                                                                 &preprocess.tokens[assignment_index - 1]);
 20595:         if (destination_id.value >= result->entity_count)
 20596:         {
 20597:             continue;
 20598:         }
 20599:         CEntity destination = result->entities[destination_id.value];
 20600:         if (destination.declaration_token_plus_one == assignment_index ||
 20601:             (destination.kind != C_ENTITY_LOCAL && destination.kind != C_ENTITY_PARAMETER && destination.kind != C_ENTITY_OBJECT) ||
 20602:             destination.type.value >= result->type_count)
 20603:         {
 20604:             continue;
 20605:         }
 20606:         u32 source_end = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, assignment_index + 1, end);
 20607:         CTypeId source_type = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 20608:         c_parse_checked_expression_type(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, assignment_scope, assignment_index + 1,
 20609:                                         source_end, &source_type, diagnostic);
 20610:         if (source_type.value < result->type_count)
 20611:         {

 20613:                                                                                  destination.type, source_type, assignment_index + 1, source_end);
 20614:             if (conversion_message.length)
 20615:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, conversion_message, assignment_index, assignment_index + 1);
 20616:         }
 20617:     }
 20618:     u32* openers = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, u32, end - start);
 20619:     u8* declaration_tokens = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, u8, end - start);
 20620:     memset(declaration_tokens, 0, end - start);
 20621:     for (u32 entity_index = first_local; entity_index < result->entity_count; entity_index = next_local[entity_index])
 20622:     {
 20623:         CEntity entity = result->entities[entity_index];
 20624:         if (entity.kind == C_ENTITY_LOCAL && entity.declaration_token_start >= start && entity.declaration_token_start < end)
 20625:         {
 20626:             u32 limit = BUSTER_MIN(end, entity.declaration_token_start + entity.declaration_token_count);
 20627:             u32 depth = 0;
 20628:             for (u32 cursor = entity.declaration_token_start; cursor < limit; cursor += 1)
 20629:             {
 20630:                 CToken token = preprocess.tokens[cursor];
 20631:                 declaration_tokens[cursor - start] = 1;
 20632:                 if (!depth && !entity.is_static_storage && c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_ASSIGN))
 20633:                 {
 20634:                     break;

 20636:                 depth += token.kind == C_TOKEN_PUNCTUATOR && c_punctuator_in_set(token.punctuator, C_PUNCTUATOR_SET_DELIMITER_OPEN);
 20637:                 depth -= depth && token.kind == C_TOKEN_PUNCTUATOR && c_punctuator_in_set(token.punctuator, C_PUNCTUATOR_SET_DELIMITER_CLOSE);
 20638:             }
 20639:         }
 20640:     }
 20641:     memset(openers, 0xff, sizeof(*openers) * (end - start));

 20645:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET) ||

 20916:         bool read_only = !member_place && !cast_place && typed && type_id.value < result->type_count && result->types[type_id.value].is_const;

 20919:         if (root < operand_end && preprocess.tokens[root].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER)

 20945:         }

 21017: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL CTypeId c_parse_constraint_function_type(CParseResult* result, CTypeId type)
 21018: {
 21019:     if (type.value < result->type_count && result->types[type.value].kind == C_TYPE_POINTER)
 21020:     {
 21021:         type = result->types[type.value].element_type;
 21022:     }
 21023:     if (type.value >= result->type_count || result->types[type.value].kind != C_TYPE_FUNCTION)
 21024:     {
 21025:         type = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 21026:     }
 21027:     return type;
 21028: }

 21030: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_incompatible_function_initializer(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result,
 21031:                                                                     CPreprocessResult preprocess, CScopeId scope, CTypeId destination,
 21032:                                                                     u32 start, u32 end)
 21033: {
 21034:     bool incompatible = false;
 21035:     CTypeId destination_function = c_parse_constraint_function_type(result, destination);
 21036:     if (destination_function.value < result->type_count)
 21037:     {

 21051: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_return_statements(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CDeclaration const* declaration,
 21052:                                                             CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 21053: {
 21054:     CTypeId return_id = declaration->type.value < result->type_count ? result->types[declaration->type.value].return_type : C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 21055:     CTypeKind return_kind = return_id.value < result->type_count ? result->types[return_id.value].kind : C_TYPE_INVALID;
 21056:     bool returns_void = return_kind == C_TYPE_VOID;
 21057:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 21058:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 21059:     CParseCandidates returns = c_parse_candidates(result, preprocess, C_PARSE_POPULATION_RETURN, C_PARSE_POPULATION_NONE, start);
 21060:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&returns, start, end); return_kind != C_TYPE_INVALID && index < end;
 21061:          index = c_parse_candidates_next(&returns, index + 1, end))
 21062:     {
 21063:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 21064:         if (token.kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER || !c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_RETURN))
 21065:         {
 21066:             continue;
 21067:         }
 21068:         bool has_value = index + 1 < end && !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON);
 21069:         if (has_value == returns_void)
 21070:         {
 21071:             String8 message = has_value ? S8("return statement has a value but the parsed function return type is void")
 21072:                                        : S8("return statement has no value but the parsed function return type is non-void");
 21073:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, message, index, index);
 21074:         }
 21075:         else if (has_value)
 21076:         {
 21077:             u32 value_end = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, index + 1, end);
 21078:             while (value_end < end && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[value_end], C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA))
 21079:                 value_end = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, value_end + 1, end);
 21080:             if (value_end >= end || !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[value_end], C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON))
 21081:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("unsupported C function-body statement or expression near 'return'"), index, index);
 21082:             CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, index);
 21083:             if (c_parse_incompatible_function_initializer(machine, result, preprocess, scope, return_id, index + 1, value_end))
 21084:             {
 21085:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("return value has an incompatible function pointer type"), index, value_end);
 21086:             }
 21087:             else
 21088:             {
 21089:                 CTypeId source = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 21090:                 if (c_parse_checked_expression_type(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, scope, index + 1, value_end, &source, diagnostic) &&
 21091:                     source.value < result->type_count)
 21092:                 {
 21093:                     CTypeKind source_kind = result->types[source.value].kind;
 21094:                     bool source_aggregate = source_kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || source_kind == C_TYPE_UNION;
 21095:                     bool target_aggregate = return_kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || return_kind == C_TYPE_UNION;
 21096:                     String8 conversion_message = c_parse_assignment_conversion_message(machine, result, preprocess, scope, return_id, source,
 21097:                                                                                         index + 1, value_end);
 21098:                     bool incompatible_aggregate = source_aggregate && target_aggregate &&
 21099:                         !c_parse_types_compatible(machine->scratch_arena, result, preprocess,
 21100:                                                   c_parse_unqualified_type(result, return_id), c_parse_unqualified_type(result, source));
 21101:                     if (source_aggregate != target_aggregate)
 21102:                     {
 21103:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, return_kind == C_TYPE_NULLPTR
 21104:                             ? S8("only a value of type nullptr_t may be converted to nullptr_t")
 21105:                             : S8("return value is incompatible with the function return type"), index, value_end);
 21106:                     }
 21107:                     else if (conversion_message.length)
 21108:                     {
 21109:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, conversion_message, index, value_end);
 21110:                     }
 21111:                     else if (incompatible_aggregate)
 21112:                     {
 21113:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("return value is incompatible with the function return type"), index, value_end);
 21114:                     }
 21115:                 }
 21116:             }
 21117:         }
 21118:     }
 21119: }

 21051: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_return_statements(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CDeclaration const* declaration,
 21052:                                                             CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 21053: {
 21054:     CTypeId return_id = declaration->type.value < result->type_count ? result->types[declaration->type.value].return_type : C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 21055:     CTypeKind return_kind = return_id.value < result->type_count ? result->types[return_id.value].kind : C_TYPE_INVALID;
 21056:     bool returns_void = return_kind == C_TYPE_VOID;
 21057:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 21058:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 21059:     CParseCandidates returns = c_parse_candidates(result, preprocess, C_PARSE_POPULATION_RETURN, C_PARSE_POPULATION_NONE, start);
 21060:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&returns, start, end); return_kind != C_TYPE_INVALID && index < end;
 21061:          index = c_parse_candidates_next(&returns, index + 1, end))
 21062:     {
 21063:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 21064:         if (token.kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER || !c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_RETURN))
 21065:         {
 21066:             continue;
 21067:         }
 21068:         bool has_value = index + 1 < end && !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON);
 21069:         if (has_value == returns_void)
 21070:         {
 21071:             String8 message = has_value ? S8("return statement has a value but the parsed function return type is void")
 21072:                                        : S8("return statement has no value but the parsed function return type is non-void");
 21073:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, message, index, index);
 21074:         }
 21075:         else if (has_value)
 21076:         {
 21077:             u32 value_end = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, index + 1, end);
 21078:             while (value_end < end && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[value_end], C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA))
 21079:                 value_end = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, value_end + 1, end);
 21080:             if (value_end >= end || !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[value_end], C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON))
 21081:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("unsupported C function-body statement or expression near 'return'"), index, index);
 21082:             CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, index);
 21083:             if (c_parse_incompatible_function_initializer(machine, result, preprocess, scope, return_id, index + 1, value_end))
 21084:             {
 21085:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("return value has an incompatible function pointer type"), index, value_end);
 21086:             }
 21087:             else
 21088:             {
 21089:                 CTypeId source = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 21090:                 if (c_parse_checked_expression_type(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, scope, index + 1, value_end, &source, diagnostic) &&
 21091:                     source.value < result->type_count)
 21092:                 {
 21093:                     CTypeKind source_kind = result->types[source.value].kind;
 21094:                     bool source_aggregate = source_kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || source_kind == C_TYPE_UNION;
 21095:                     bool target_aggregate = return_kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || return_kind == C_TYPE_UNION;
 21096:                     String8 conversion_message = c_parse_assignment_conversion_message(machine, result, preprocess, scope, return_id, source,
 21097:                                                                                         index + 1, value_end);
 21098:                     bool incompatible_aggregate = source_aggregate && target_aggregate &&
 21099:                         !c_parse_types_compatible(machine->scratch_arena, result, preprocess,
 21100:                                                   c_parse_unqualified_type(result, return_id), c_parse_unqualified_type(result, source));
 21101:                     if (source_aggregate != target_aggregate)
 21102:                     {
 21103:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, return_kind == C_TYPE_NULLPTR
 21104:                             ? S8("only a value of type nullptr_t may be converted to nullptr_t")
 21105:                             : S8("return value is incompatible with the function return type"), index, value_end);
 21106:                     }
 21107:                     else if (conversion_message.length)
 21108:                     {
 21109:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, conversion_message, index, value_end);
 21110:                     }
 21111:                     else if (incompatible_aggregate)
 21112:                     {
 21113:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("return value is incompatible with the function return type"), index, value_end);
 21114:                     }
 21115:                 }
 21116:             }
 21117:         }
 21118:     }
 21119: }

 21122:                                                 CDeclaration const* declaration, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 21123: {
 21124:     BUSTER_UNUSED(result);
 21125:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 21126:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 21127:     u32 count = 0;
 21128:     CParseCandidates label_candidates = c_parse_candidates(result, preprocess, C_PARSE_POPULATION_LABEL_CANDIDATES, C_PARSE_POPULATION_NONE, start);
 21129:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&label_candidates, start, end); index + 1 < end;
 21130:          index = c_parse_candidates_next(&label_candidates, index + 1, end))
 21131:     {
 21132:         count += c_ir_named_label_at(&preprocess, start, index, end);
 21133:     }
 21134:     u64 capacity = 1;
 21135:     while (capacity < (u64)count * 2)

@@ 21030:21140 @@
 21030: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_incompatible_function_initializer(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result,
 21031:                                                                     CPreprocessResult preprocess, CScopeId scope, CTypeId destination,
 21032:                                                                     u32 start, u32 end)
 21033: {
 21034:     bool incompatible = false;
 21035:     CTypeId destination_function = c_parse_constraint_function_type(result, destination);
 21036:     if (destination_function.value < result->type_count)
 21037:     {
 21038:         u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 21039:         CTypeId source = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 21040:         if (c_parse_expression_type_query(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, scope, start, end, &source))
 21041:         {
 21042:             CTypeId source_function = c_parse_constraint_function_type(result, source);
 21043:             incompatible = source_function.value < result->type_count &&
 21044:                            !c_parse_types_compatible(machine->scratch_arena, result, preprocess, destination_function, source_function);
 21045:         }
 21046:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 21047:     }
 21048:     return incompatible;
 21049: }

 21051: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_return_statements(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CDeclaration const* declaration,
 21052:                                                             CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 21053: {
 21054:     CTypeId return_id = declaration->type.value < result->type_count ? result->types[declaration->type.value].return_type : C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 21055:     CTypeKind return_kind = return_id.value < result->type_count ? result->types[return_id.value].kind : C_TYPE_INVALID;
 21056:     bool returns_void = return_kind == C_TYPE_VOID;
 21057:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 21058:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 21059:     CParseCandidates returns = c_parse_candidates(result, preprocess, C_PARSE_POPULATION_RETURN, C_PARSE_POPULATION_NONE, start);
 21060:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&returns, start, end); return_kind != C_TYPE_INVALID && index < end;
 21061:          index = c_parse_candidates_next(&returns, index + 1, end))
 21062:     {
 21063:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 21064:         if (token.kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER || !c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_RETURN))
 21065:         {
 21066:             continue;
 21067:         }
 21068:         bool has_value = index + 1 < end && !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON);
 21069:         if (has_value == returns_void)
 21070:         {
 21071:             String8 message = has_value ? S8("return statement has a value but the parsed function return type is void")
 21072:                                        : S8("return statement has no value but the parsed function return type is non-void");
 21073:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, message, index, index);
 21074:         }
 21075:         else if (has_value)
 21076:         {
 21077:             u32 value_end = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, index + 1, end);
 21078:             while (value_end < end && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[value_end], C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA))
 21079:                 value_end = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, value_end + 1, end);
 21080:             if (value_end >= end || !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[value_end], C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON))
 21081:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("unsupported C function-body statement or expression near 'return'"), index, index);
 21082:             CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, index);
 21083:             if (c_parse_incompatible_function_initializer(machine, result, preprocess, scope, return_id, index + 1, value_end))
 21084:             {
 21085:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("return value has an incompatible function pointer type"), index, value_end);
 21086:             }
 21087:             else
 21088:             {
 21089:                 CTypeId source = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 21090:                 if (c_parse_checked_expression_type(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, scope, index + 1, value_end, &source, diagnostic) &&
 21091:                     source.value < result->type_count)
 21092:                 {
 21093:                     CTypeKind source_kind = result->types[source.value].kind;
 21094:                     bool source_aggregate = source_kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || source_kind == C_TYPE_UNION;
 21095:                     bool target_aggregate = return_kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || return_kind == C_TYPE_UNION;
 21096:                     String8 conversion_message = c_parse_assignment_conversion_message(machine, result, preprocess, scope, return_id, source,
 21097:                                                                                         index + 1, value_end);
 21098:                     bool incompatible_aggregate = source_aggregate && target_aggregate &&
 21099:                         !c_parse_types_compatible(machine->scratch_arena, result, preprocess,
 21100:                                                   c_parse_unqualified_type(result, return_id), c_parse_unqualified_type(result, source));
 21101:                     if (source_aggregate != target_aggregate)
 21102:                     {
 21103:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, return_kind == C_TYPE_NULLPTR
 21104:                             ? S8("only a value of type nullptr_t may be converted to nullptr_t")
 21105:                             : S8("return value is incompatible with the function return type"), index, value_end);
 21106:                     }
 21107:                     else if (conversion_message.length)
 21108:                     {
 21109:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, conversion_message, index, value_end);
 21110:                     }
 21111:                     else if (incompatible_aggregate)
 21112:                     {
 21113:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("return value is incompatible with the function return type"), index, value_end);
 21114:                     }
 21115:                 }
 21116:             }
 21117:         }
 21118:     }
 21119: }

 21121: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_labels(CTypeParseMachine* machine, Arena* arena, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 21122:                                                 CDeclaration const* declaration, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 21123: {
 21124:     BUSTER_UNUSED(result);
 21125:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 21126:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 21127:     u32 count = 0;
 21128:     CParseCandidates label_candidates = c_parse_candidates(result, preprocess, C_PARSE_POPULATION_LABEL_CANDIDATES, C_PARSE_POPULATION_NONE, start);
 21129:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&label_candidates, start, end); index + 1 < end;
 21130:          index = c_parse_candidates_next(&label_candidates, index + 1, end))
 21131:     {
 21132:         count += c_ir_named_label_at(&preprocess, start, index, end);
 21133:     }
 21134:     u64 capacity = 1;
 21135:     while (capacity < (u64)count * 2)
 21136:     {
 21137:         capacity *= 2;
 21138:     }
 21139:     u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;

 21141:     memset(labels, 0, sizeof(*labels) * capacity);
 21142:     label_candidates = c_parse_candidates(result, preprocess, C_PARSE_POPULATION_LABEL_CANDIDATES, C_PARSE_POPULATION_NONE, start);

 21144:          index = c_parse_candidates_next(&label_candidates, index + 1, end))

 21065:         {

 21067:         }

 21074:         }

 21078:             while (value_end < end && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[value_end], C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA))

 21097:                                                                                         index + 1, value_end);

 21110:                     }

 21121: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_labels(CTypeParseMachine* machine, Arena* arena, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 21122:                                                 CDeclaration const* declaration, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 21123: {
 21124:     BUSTER_UNUSED(result);
 21125:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 21126:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 21127:     u32 count = 0;

 21157:             }
 21158:             else
 21159:             {
 21160:                 labels[slot] = index + 1;
 21161:             }
 21162:         }

 21165:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&jumps, start, end); index + 1 < end; index = c_parse_candidates_next(&jumps, index + 1, end))
 21166:     {
 21167:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];

 21143:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&label_candidates, start, end); index + 1 < end;
 21144:          index = c_parse_candidates_next(&label_candidates, index + 1, end))
 21145:     {
 21146:         if (c_ir_named_label_at(&preprocess, start, index, end))

 21149:             u64 slot = c_macro_name_hash(name) & (capacity - 1);
 21150:             while (labels[slot] && !string_equal(name, c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[labels[slot] - 1])))
 21151:             {
 21152:                 slot = (slot + 1) & (capacity - 1);
 21153:             }
 21154:             if (labels[slot])
 21155:             {
 21156:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, string_format(arena, S8("duplicate label '{S8}'"), name), index, index);
 21157:             }
 21158:             else
 21159:             {
 21160:                 labels[slot] = index + 1;
 21161:             }
 21162:         }
 21163:     }

 21165:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&jumps, start, end); index + 1 < end; index = c_parse_candidates_next(&jumps, index + 1, end))
 21166:     {
 21167:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 21168:         bool named_goto = token.kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER && c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_GOTO);
 21169:         bool label_address = c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_AMPERSAND_AMPERSAND) &&

 21171:         if (named_goto && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON))
 21172:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("malformed goto statement"), index, index);
 21173:         if ((named_goto || label_address) && preprocess.tokens[index + 1].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER)
 21174:         {

 21176:             u64 slot = c_macro_name_hash(name) & (capacity - 1);
 21177:             while (labels[slot] && !string_equal(name, c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[labels[slot] - 1])))
 21178:             {
 21179:                 slot = (slot + 1) & (capacity - 1);
 21180:             }
 21181:             if (!labels[slot])
 21182:             {
 21183:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, string_format(arena, S8("label '{S8}' is not defined in this function"), name),
 21184:                                                      index, index + 1);
 21185:             }
 21186:         }
 21187:     }
 21188:     arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 21189: }

 21219:                 variable = !value.valid;
 21220:                 arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 21221:             }
 21222:         }
 21223:         type_id = type.element_type;
 21224:     }
 21225:     return variable;

 21203: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_local_type_is_variable_length(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 21204:                                                                CScopeId scope, CTypeId type_id)

 21237:         {

 21344:     return !checked.message.length;

 21949: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_atomic_accesses(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 21950:                                                          CDeclaration const* declaration, u8 const* skipped, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 21951: {
 21952:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 21953:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 21954:     for (u32 index = start; index < end; index += 1)
 21955:     {
 21956:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 21957:         if (skipped[index - start] || token.kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER) continue;
 21958:         if (c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_SIZEOF) && index + 1 < end &&
 21959:             c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 21960:         {
 21961:             u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, index + 1);
 21962:             if (close < end) index = close;
 21963:             continue;
 21964:         }
 21965:         u32 use = c_parse_identifier_use_index(result, index);
 21966:         CEntityId entity_id = use != C_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID ? result->identifier_uses[use].entity : C_ENTITY_ID_INVALID;
 21967:         if (entity_id.value >= result->entity_count) continue;
 21968:         CEntity entity = result->entities[entity_id.value];
 21969:         if (entity.kind != C_ENTITY_OBJECT && entity.kind != C_ENTITY_LOCAL && entity.kind != C_ENTITY_PARAMETER) continue;
 21970:         if (entity.type.value >= result->type_count || !result->types[entity.type.value].is_atomic ||
 21971:             entity.declaration_token_plus_one == index + 1 || (index > start && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_AMPERSAND))) continue;
 21972:         CType type = result->types[entity.type.value];
 21973:         bool store = index + 1 < end && c_parse_assignment_punctuator(preprocess.tokens[index + 1]);
 21974:         String8 message = {0};
 21975:         u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 21976:         if (c_parse_type_contains_wide_float(machine, result, preprocess.target, entity.type))
 21977:             message = store ? S8("C IR lowering does not yet support atomic wide floating-point stores")
 21978:                             : S8("C IR lowering does not yet support atomic wide floating-point loads");
 21979:         else if (type.kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || type.kind == C_TYPE_UNION)
 21980:         {
 21981:             u64 size = 0;
 21982:             u32 alignment = 0;
 21983:             if (c_parse_type_layout(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, entity.type, &size, &alignment) &&
 21984:                 size > target_data_layout(preprocess.target).atomic_max_width / 8)
 21985:                 message = string_format(result->arena, S8("C IR lowering does not support an atomic {S8} of a {u64}-byte aggregate: this target has no lock-free access that wide"),
 21986:                     store ? S8("store") : S8("load"), size);
 21987:         }
 21988:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 21989:         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, message, index, index);
 21990:     }
 21991: }

 21993: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_vla_declarations(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 21994:                                                            u32 declaration_index, u32 first_local, u32 const* next_local, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 21995: {
 21996:     for (u32 entity_index = first_local; entity_index < result->entity_count; entity_index = next_local[entity_index])
 21997:     {
 21998:         CEntity* entity = result->entities + entity_index;
 21999:         if (entity->kind != C_ENTITY_LOCAL || entity->declaration_index != declaration_index ||
 22000:             entity->declaration_token_start >= preprocess.token_count || !entity->declaration_token_count)
 22001:         {
 22002:             continue;
 22003:         }
 22004:         u32 start = entity->declaration_token_start;
 22005:         u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + entity->declaration_token_count);
 22006:         bool previous_runtime = machine->runtime_expression_constraints;
 22007:         machine->runtime_expression_constraints = !entity->is_static_storage;
 22008:         u32 body_start = declaration_index < result->declaration_count ? result->declarations[declaration_index].body_start : UINT32_MAX;
 22009:         u32 location = entity->declaration_statement_start >= body_start && entity->declaration_statement_start <= start
 22010:                            ? entity->declaration_statement_start
 22011:                            : start;
 22012:         if (location < preprocess.token_count && string_equal(c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[location]), S8("__attribute__")))
 22013:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("could not lower unbound identifier '__attribute__'"), location, location);
 22014:         String8 alignment_message = c_parse_validate_alignment_range(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type,
 22015:                                                                       entity->alignment_start, entity->alignment_count, 0);
 22016:         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider_kind(diagnostic, alignment_message, C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_ALIGNMENT, start, location);
 22017:         if (entity->type.value < result->type_count && result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_VOID)
 22018:         {
 22019:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider_kind(diagnostic, S8("a variable may not have type 'void'"),
 22020:                                                        C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_VOID_OBJECT, start, location);
 22021:         }
 22022:         else if (entity->type.value < result->type_count && result->types[entity->type.value].is_atomic &&
 22023:                  result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE && target_data_layout(preprocess.target).long_double_type.bit_width > 64)
 22024:         {
 22025:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("C IR lowering does not yet support atomic wide floating-point objects"), start, location);
 22026:         }
 22027:         else if (c_parse_local_type_is_variable_length(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type) && entity->is_static_storage)
 22028:         {

 22030:         }

 22032:                  c_parse_declarator_has_initializer(preprocess, start, end))
 22033:         {
 22034:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("variable-length array cannot have an initializer"), start, location);
 22035:         }
 22036:         else if (entity->type.value < result->type_count &&
 22037:                  (result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_UNION) &&
 22038:                  !result->types[entity->type.value].is_complete)
 22039:         {
 22040:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("local declaration has an invalid alignment"), start, location);
 22041:         }

 22043:         u32 shape_start = 0;
 22044:         u32 shape_end = 0;
@@ 21958:22068 @@
 21958:         if (c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_SIZEOF) && index + 1 < end &&
 21959:             c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 21960:         {
 21961:             u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, index + 1);
 21962:             if (close < end) index = close;
 21963:             continue;
 21964:         }
 21965:         u32 use = c_parse_identifier_use_index(result, index);
 21966:         CEntityId entity_id = use != C_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID ? result->identifier_uses[use].entity : C_ENTITY_ID_INVALID;
 21967:         if (entity_id.value >= result->entity_count) continue;
 21968:         CEntity entity = result->entities[entity_id.value];
 21969:         if (entity.kind != C_ENTITY_OBJECT && entity.kind != C_ENTITY_LOCAL && entity.kind != C_ENTITY_PARAMETER) continue;
 21970:         if (entity.type.value >= result->type_count || !result->types[entity.type.value].is_atomic ||
 21971:             entity.declaration_token_plus_one == index + 1 || (index > start && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_AMPERSAND))) continue;
 21972:         CType type = result->types[entity.type.value];
 21973:         bool store = index + 1 < end && c_parse_assignment_punctuator(preprocess.tokens[index + 1]);
 21974:         String8 message = {0};
 21975:         u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 21976:         if (c_parse_type_contains_wide_float(machine, result, preprocess.target, entity.type))
 21977:             message = store ? S8("C IR lowering does not yet support atomic wide floating-point stores")
 21978:                             : S8("C IR lowering does not yet support atomic wide floating-point loads");
 21979:         else if (type.kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || type.kind == C_TYPE_UNION)
 21980:         {
 21981:             u64 size = 0;
 21982:             u32 alignment = 0;
 21983:             if (c_parse_type_layout(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, entity.type, &size, &alignment) &&
 21984:                 size > target_data_layout(preprocess.target).atomic_max_width / 8)
 21985:                 message = string_format(result->arena, S8("C IR lowering does not support an atomic {S8} of a {u64}-byte aggregate: this target has no lock-free access that wide"),
 21986:                     store ? S8("store") : S8("load"), size);
 21987:         }
 21988:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 21989:         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, message, index, index);
 21990:     }

 21993: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_vla_declarations(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 21994:                                                            u32 declaration_index, u32 first_local, u32 const* next_local, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 21995: {
 21996:     for (u32 entity_index = first_local; entity_index < result->entity_count; entity_index = next_local[entity_index])
 21997:     {
 21998:         CEntity* entity = result->entities + entity_index;
 21999:         if (entity->kind != C_ENTITY_LOCAL || entity->declaration_index != declaration_index ||
 22000:             entity->declaration_token_start >= preprocess.token_count || !entity->declaration_token_count)
 22001:         {
 22002:             continue;
 22003:         }
 22004:         u32 start = entity->declaration_token_start;
 22005:         u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + entity->declaration_token_count);
 22006:         bool previous_runtime = machine->runtime_expression_constraints;
 22007:         machine->runtime_expression_constraints = !entity->is_static_storage;
 22008:         u32 body_start = declaration_index < result->declaration_count ? result->declarations[declaration_index].body_start : UINT32_MAX;
 22009:         u32 location = entity->declaration_statement_start >= body_start && entity->declaration_statement_start <= start
 22010:                            ? entity->declaration_statement_start
 22011:                            : start;
 22012:         if (location < preprocess.token_count && string_equal(c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[location]), S8("__attribute__")))
 22013:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("could not lower unbound identifier '__attribute__'"), location, location);
 22014:         String8 alignment_message = c_parse_validate_alignment_range(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type,
 22015:                                                                       entity->alignment_start, entity->alignment_count, 0);
 22016:         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider_kind(diagnostic, alignment_message, C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_ALIGNMENT, start, location);
 22017:         if (entity->type.value < result->type_count && result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_VOID)
 22018:         {
 22019:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider_kind(diagnostic, S8("a variable may not have type 'void'"),
 22020:                                                        C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_VOID_OBJECT, start, location);
 22021:         }
 22022:         else if (entity->type.value < result->type_count && result->types[entity->type.value].is_atomic &&
 22023:                  result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE && target_data_layout(preprocess.target).long_double_type.bit_width > 64)
 22024:         {
 22025:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("C IR lowering does not yet support atomic wide floating-point objects"), start, location);
 22026:         }
 22027:         else if (c_parse_local_type_is_variable_length(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type) && entity->is_static_storage)
 22028:         {
 22029:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("variable-length array cannot have static storage duration"), start, location);
 22030:         }
 22031:         else if (c_parse_local_type_is_variable_length(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type) &&
 22032:                  c_parse_declarator_has_initializer(preprocess, start, end))
 22033:         {
 22034:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("variable-length array cannot have an initializer"), start, location);
 22035:         }
 22036:         else if (entity->type.value < result->type_count &&
 22037:                  (result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_UNION) &&
 22038:                  !result->types[entity->type.value].is_complete)
 22039:         {
 22040:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("local declaration has an invalid alignment"), start, location);
 22041:         }
 22042:         CDeclaration local_initializer = {.declarator_start = start, .declarator_count = end - start};
 22043:         u32 shape_start = 0;
 22044:         u32 shape_end = 0;
 22045:         if (c_ir_declaration_initializer_range(preprocess, local_initializer, &shape_start, &shape_end))
 22046:         {
 22047:             bool braced_initializer = shape_start < shape_end &&
 22048:                                      c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[shape_start], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE);
 22049:             String8 simple_conversion_message = {0};
 22050:             u64 conversion_mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22051:             bool simple_conversion_failure = !entity->is_static_storage && !braced_initializer &&
 22052:                 c_parse_incompatible_aggregate_value(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type,
 22053:                                                      shape_start, shape_end, true, &simple_conversion_message) &&
 22054:                 simple_conversion_message.length;
 22055:             arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, conversion_mark);
 22056:             CParseInitializerDiagnostic shape = simple_conversion_failure
 22057:                 ? (CParseInitializerDiagnostic){.message = simple_conversion_message, .token = shape_start, .container = entity->type}
 22058:                 : c_parse_validate_initializer_shape(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type, shape_start, shape_end);
 22059:             if (!simple_conversion_failure && entity->type.value < result->type_count && result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_NULLPTR &&
 22060:                 c_parse_incompatible_aggregate_value(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type, shape_start, shape_end, true, 0))
 22061:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("only a value of type nullptr_t may be converted to nullptr_t"), start, shape_end);
 22062:             if (!entity->is_static_storage)
 22063:             {
 22064:                 for (u32 token = shape_start; token < shape_end; token += 1)
 22065:                 {
 22066:                     if (c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[token], C_PUNCTUATOR_ELLIPSIS))
 22067:                     {
 22068:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("range designators are only supported for static aggregate initializers"), start, location);
@@ 21961:22071 @@

 21966:         CEntityId entity_id = use != C_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID ? result->identifier_uses[use].entity : C_ENTITY_ID_INVALID;
 21967:         if (entity_id.value >= result->entity_count) continue;

 21993: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_vla_declarations(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 21994:                                                            u32 declaration_index, u32 first_local, u32 const* next_local, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 21995: {
 21996:     for (u32 entity_index = first_local; entity_index < result->entity_count; entity_index = next_local[entity_index])
 21997:     {
 21998:         CEntity* entity = result->entities + entity_index;
 21999:         if (entity->kind != C_ENTITY_LOCAL || entity->declaration_index != declaration_index ||
 22000:             entity->declaration_token_start >= preprocess.token_count || !entity->declaration_token_count)
 22001:         {
 22002:             continue;
 22003:         }
 22004:         u32 start = entity->declaration_token_start;
 22005:         u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + entity->declaration_token_count);
 22006:         bool previous_runtime = machine->runtime_expression_constraints;
 22007:         machine->runtime_expression_constraints = !entity->is_static_storage;
 22008:         u32 body_start = declaration_index < result->declaration_count ? result->declarations[declaration_index].body_start : UINT32_MAX;
 22009:         u32 location = entity->declaration_statement_start >= body_start && entity->declaration_statement_start <= start
 22010:                            ? entity->declaration_statement_start
 22011:                            : start;
 22012:         if (location < preprocess.token_count && string_equal(c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[location]), S8("__attribute__")))
 22013:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("could not lower unbound identifier '__attribute__'"), location, location);
 22014:         String8 alignment_message = c_parse_validate_alignment_range(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type,
 22015:                                                                       entity->alignment_start, entity->alignment_count, 0);
 22016:         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider_kind(diagnostic, alignment_message, C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_ALIGNMENT, start, location);
 22017:         if (entity->type.value < result->type_count && result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_VOID)
 22018:         {
 22019:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider_kind(diagnostic, S8("a variable may not have type 'void'"),
 22020:                                                        C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_VOID_OBJECT, start, location);
 22021:         }
 22022:         else if (entity->type.value < result->type_count && result->types[entity->type.value].is_atomic &&
 22023:                  result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE && target_data_layout(preprocess.target).long_double_type.bit_width > 64)
 22024:         {
 22025:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("C IR lowering does not yet support atomic wide floating-point objects"), start, location);
 22026:         }
 22027:         else if (c_parse_local_type_is_variable_length(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type) && entity->is_static_storage)
 22028:         {
 22029:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("variable-length array cannot have static storage duration"), start, location);
 22030:         }
 22031:         else if (c_parse_local_type_is_variable_length(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type) &&
 22032:                  c_parse_declarator_has_initializer(preprocess, start, end))
 22033:         {
 22034:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("variable-length array cannot have an initializer"), start, location);
 22035:         }
 22036:         else if (entity->type.value < result->type_count &&
 22037:                  (result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_UNION) &&
 22038:                  !result->types[entity->type.value].is_complete)
 22039:         {
 22040:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("local declaration has an invalid alignment"), start, location);
 22041:         }
 22042:         CDeclaration local_initializer = {.declarator_start = start, .declarator_count = end - start};
 22043:         u32 shape_start = 0;
 22044:         u32 shape_end = 0;
 22045:         if (c_ir_declaration_initializer_range(preprocess, local_initializer, &shape_start, &shape_end))
 22046:         {
 22047:             bool braced_initializer = shape_start < shape_end &&
 22048:                                      c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[shape_start], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE);
 22049:             String8 simple_conversion_message = {0};
 22050:             u64 conversion_mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22051:             bool simple_conversion_failure = !entity->is_static_storage && !braced_initializer &&
 22052:                 c_parse_incompatible_aggregate_value(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type,
 22053:                                                      shape_start, shape_end, true, &simple_conversion_message) &&
 22054:                 simple_conversion_message.length;
 22055:             arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, conversion_mark);
 22056:             CParseInitializerDiagnostic shape = simple_conversion_failure
 22057:                 ? (CParseInitializerDiagnostic){.message = simple_conversion_message, .token = shape_start, .container = entity->type}
 22058:                 : c_parse_validate_initializer_shape(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type, shape_start, shape_end);
 22059:             if (!simple_conversion_failure && entity->type.value < result->type_count && result->types[entity->type.value].kind == C_TYPE_NULLPTR &&
 22060:                 c_parse_incompatible_aggregate_value(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type, shape_start, shape_end, true, 0))
 22061:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("only a value of type nullptr_t may be converted to nullptr_t"), start, shape_end);
 22062:             if (!entity->is_static_storage)
 22063:             {
 22064:                 for (u32 token = shape_start; token < shape_end; token += 1)
 22065:                 {
 22066:                     if (c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[token], C_PUNCTUATOR_ELLIPSIS))
 22067:                     {
 22068:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("range designators are only supported for static aggregate initializers"), start, location);
 22069:                     }
 22070:                 }

 22073:             {

 22127:                         String8 message = string_format(result->arena, S8("could not lower static initializer for local '{S8}'"), entity->name);
 22128:                         if (call < preprocess.token_count && c_symbol_builtin_from_spelling(c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[call])) == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_NONE)
 22129:                         {
 22130:                             message = string_format(result->arena,
 22131:                                 S8("could not lower static initializer for local '{S8}': cannot fold the call to '{S8}' in a static initializer"),
 22132:                                 entity->name, c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[call]));
 22133:                             function_name = call;
 22134:                         }
 22135:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, message, start, function_name);
 22136:                     }
 22137:                 }
 22138:             }
 22139:         }
 22140:         bool is_extern = false;
 22141:         for (u32 specifier = location; specifier < start; specifier += 1)
 22142:         {
 22143:             is_extern |= c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[specifier], C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_EXTERN);
 22144:         }
 22145:         if (is_extern && c_parse_declarator_has_initializer(preprocess, start, end))
 22146:         {
 22147:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("local extern declarations cannot allocate an initialized automatic object"), start, location);
 22148:         }
 22149:         {
 22150:             for (u32 cursor = start; cursor < end; cursor += 1)
 22151:             {
 22152:                 CToken token = preprocess.tokens[cursor];
 22153:                 if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET))
 22154:                 {
 22155:                     u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, cursor);
 22156:                     cursor = close < end ? close : end;
 22157:                 }
 22158:                 else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_ASSIGN))
 22159:                 {
 22160:                     u32 value_end = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, cursor + 1, end);
 22161:                     if (c_parse_incompatible_function_initializer(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type, cursor + 1, value_end))
 22162:                     {
 22163:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("assignment from an incompatible function pointer type"), cursor, cursor + 1);
 22164:                     }
 22165:                     if (!c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[cursor + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE))
 22166:                     {
 22167:                         CTypeId initializer_type = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 22168:                         c_parse_checked_expression_type(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, entity->scope,
 22169:                             cursor + 1, value_end, &initializer_type, diagnostic);
 22170:                         String8 conversion_message = {0};
 22171:                         if (c_parse_incompatible_aggregate_value(machine, result, preprocess, entity->scope, entity->type,
 22172:                                                                   cursor + 1, value_end, true, &conversion_message))
 22173:                             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic,
 22174:                                 conversion_message.length ? conversion_message : S8("initializer has an incompatible type"), cursor, value_end);
 22175:                     }
 22176:                     break;
 22177:                 }
 22178:             }
 22179:         }
 22180:         machine->runtime_expression_constraints = previous_runtime;
 22181:     }
 22182: }

 22200:         {

 22211:                 index = nested < close ? nested : close;

 22218:         if (controller_end == UINT32_MAX)
 22219:         {
 22220:             continue;
 22221:         }
 22222: 
 22223:         CScopeId generic_scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, generic);
 22224:         u64 scratch_mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22225:         CTypeId controller_type = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 22226:         bool controller_resolved = c_parse_expression_type_query(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, generic_scope, generic + 2,
 22227:                                                                  controller_end, &controller_type);
 22228:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, scratch_mark);
 22229:         if (controller_resolved)
 22230:         {
 22231:             controller_type = c_parse_auto_decay_type(result, controller_type);
 22232:         }
 22233: 
 22234:         u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22235:         CTypeId* association_types = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, CTypeId, close - controller_end + 1);
 22236:         u32 association_type_count = 0;
 22237:         u32 match_count = 0;
 22238:         u32 default_count = 0;
 22239:         bool generic_valid = true;
 22240:         u32 association_start = controller_end + 1;
 22241:         while (association_start < close && generic_valid)
 22242:         {
 22243:             u32 colon = UINT32_MAX;
 22244:             u32 association_end = close;
 22245:             for (u32 index = association_start; index < close; index += 1)
 22246:             {
 22247:                 CToken current = preprocess.tokens[index];
 22248:                 if (c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS) ||
 22249:                     c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET) || c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE))
 22250:                 {
 22251:                     u32 nested = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, index);
 22252:                     index = nested < close ? nested : close;
 22253:                 }
 22254:                 else if (colon == UINT32_MAX && c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_COLON))
 22255:                 {
 22256:                     colon = index;
 22257:                 }
 22258:                 else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA))
 22259:                 {
 22260:                     association_end = index;
 22261:                     break;
 22262:                 }
 22263:             }
 22264:             if (colon == UINT32_MAX || colon == association_start || colon + 1 >= association_end)
 22265:             {
 22266:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("expected 'type-name: expression' or 'default: expression' in _Generic association"),
 22267:                                                      association_start, association_start);
 22268:                 generic_valid = false;
 22269:                 continue;
 22270:             }
 22271:             bool is_default = colon == association_start + 1 && preprocess.tokens[association_start].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER &&
 22272:                               string_equal(c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[association_start]), S8("default"));
 22273:             if (is_default)
 22274:             {
 22275:                 default_count += 1;
 22276:                 if (default_count > 1)
 22277:                 {
 22278:                     c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("_Generic selection has more than one default association"),
 22279:                                                          association_start, association_start);
 22280:                     generic_valid = false;
 22281:                 }
 22282:             }
 22283:             else
 22284:             {
 22285:                 CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, association_start);
 22286:                 u32 type_index = association_start;
 22287:                 CTypeId type = c_parse_machineless_base_type(result, preprocess, scope, association_start, colon, &type_index);
 22288:                 if (type.value < result->type_count)
 22289:                 {
 22290:                     type = c_parse_pointer_chain(result, preprocess, type, &type_index, colon);
 22291:                     type = c_parse_array_suffixes(result, preprocess, type, &type_index, colon);
 22292:                     if (type_index < colon && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[type_index], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 22293:                     {
 22294:                         type = c_parse_local_function_suffix(machine, result, preprocess, type, type_index, colon, &type_index);
 22295:                     }
 22296:                 }
 22297:                 if (type.value < result->type_count && type_index == colon)
 22298:                 {
 22299:                     CTypeKind kind = result->types[type.value].kind;
 22300:                     u64 layout_mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22301:                     u64 size = 0;
 22302:                     u32 alignment = 0;
 22303:                     bool complete_object = kind != C_TYPE_VOID && kind != C_TYPE_FUNCTION &&
 22304:                                            c_parse_type_layout(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, type, &size, &alignment);
 22305:                     arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, layout_mark);
 22306:                     BUSTER_UNUSED(size);
 22307:                     BUSTER_UNUSED(alignment);
 22308:                     if (!complete_object)
 22309:                     {
 22310:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("_Generic association requires a complete object type"),
 22311:                                                              association_start, association_start);
 22312:                         generic_valid = false;
 22313:                     }
 22314:                     for (u32 previous = 0; generic_valid && previous < association_type_count; previous += 1)
 22315:                     {

 22317:                         {
 22318:                             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("_Generic selection has multiple compatible type associations"),

 22308:                     if (!complete_object)
 22309:                     {
 22310:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("_Generic association requires a complete object type"),
 22311:                                                              association_start, association_start);
 22312:                         generic_valid = false;
 22313:                     }
 22314:                     for (u32 previous = 0; generic_valid && previous < association_type_count; previous += 1)
 22315:                     {
 22316:                         if (c_parse_types_compatible_core(arena, result, preprocess, association_types[previous], type, true))
 22317:                         {
 22318:                             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("_Generic selection has multiple compatible type associations"),
 22319:                                                                  association_start, association_start);
 22320:                             generic_valid = false;
 22321:                         }
 22322:                     }
 22323:                     if (generic_valid)
 22324:                     {
 22325:                         association_types[association_type_count++] = type;
 22326:                         match_count += controller_resolved && controller_type.value < result->type_count &&
 22327:                                        c_parse_types_compatible_core(arena, result, preprocess, controller_type, type, true);
 22328:                     }
 22329:                 }
 22330:             }
 22331:             if (association_end == close)
 22332:             {
 22333:                 break;
 22334:             }
 22335:             association_start = association_end + 1;
 22336:         }
 22337:         if (generic_valid && controller_resolved && !match_count && !default_count)
 22338:         {
 22339:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(
 22340:                 diagnostic, S8("_Generic controlling type is not compatible with any association and no default was provided"), generic, generic);
 22341:         }
 22342:         if (generic_valid && skipped)
 22343:         {
 22344:             u32 selected_start = 0;
 22345:             u32 selected_end = 0;
 22346:             u32 consumed = 0;
 22347:             if (c_parse_generic_selection_range(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, generic_scope, generic, end,
 22348:                                                 &selected_start, &selected_end, &consumed))
 22349:             {
 22350:                 memset(skipped + generic + 1 - start, 1, selected_start - generic - 1);
 22351:                 memset(skipped + selected_end - start, 1, consumed - selected_end);
 22352:             }
 22353:         }
 22354:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 22355:     }
 22356: }

 22358: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL u32 c_parse_case_label_colon(CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess, u32 start, u32 end)
 22359: {
 22360:     u32 colon = UINT32_MAX;
 22361:     u32 questions = 0;
 22362:     u32 index = start;
 22363:     while (index < end && colon == UINT32_MAX)
 22364:     {
 22365:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 22366:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET) ||
 22367:             c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE))
 22368:         {
 22369:             u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, index);
 22370:             index = close < end ? close + 1 : end;
 22371:         }
 22372:         else
 22373:         {
 22374:             if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_QUESTION))
 22375:             {
 22376:                 questions += 1;
 22377:             }
 22378:             else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_COLON))
 22379:             {
 22380:                 if (questions)
 22381:                 {
 22382:                     questions -= 1;
 22383:                 }
 22384:                 else
 22385:                 {
 22386:                     colon = index;
 22387:                 }
 22388:             }
 22389:             index += 1;
 22390:         }
 22391:     }
 22392:     return colon;
 22393: }

 22353:         }
 22354:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 22355:     }
 22356: }
