 22358: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL u32 c_parse_case_label_colon(CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess, u32 start, u32 end)
 22359: {
 22360:     u32 colon = UINT32_MAX;
 22361:     u32 questions = 0;
 22362:     u32 index = start;
 22363:     while (index < end && colon == UINT32_MAX)
 22364:     {
 22365:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 22366:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET) ||
 22367:             c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE))
 22368:         {
 22369:             u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, index);
 22370:             index = close < end ? close + 1 : end;
 22371:         }
 22372:         else
 22373:         {
 22374:             if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_QUESTION))
 22375:             {
 22376:                 questions += 1;
 22377:             }
 22378:             else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_COLON))
 22379:             {
 22380:                 if (questions)
 22381:                 {
 22382:                     questions -= 1;
 22383:                 }
 22384:                 else
 22385:                 {
 22386:                     colon = index;
 22387:                 }
 22388:             }
 22389:             index += 1;
 22390:         }
 22391:     }
 22392:     return colon;
 22393: }

 22395: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_one_switch(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 22396:                                                      CDeclaration const* declaration, u32 switch_index, u32 function_end, u8* suffix,
 22397:                                                      CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 22398: {
 22399:     bool header = switch_index + 1 < function_end &&
 22400:                   c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[switch_index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS);
 22401:     u32 header_close = header ? c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, switch_index + 1) : function_end;
 22402:     u32 switch_end = header_close < function_end && header_close + 1 < function_end
 22403:         ? c_parse_statement_end(preprocess, header_close + 1, function_end, suffix, function_end - header_close) : UINT32_MAX;
 22404:     bool braced_body = header_close < function_end && header_close + 1 < function_end &&
 22405:                        c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[header_close + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE);
 22406:     if (!braced_body)
 22407:     {
 22408:         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("unsupported C function-body statement or expression near 'switch'"),
 22409:                                              switch_index, switch_index);
 22410:     }
 22411:     else if (switch_end != UINT32_MAX)
 22412:     {
 22413:         CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, switch_index);
 22414:         u64 controlling_mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22415:         CTypeId controlling_type = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 22416:         bool controlling_type_resolved =
 22417:             c_parse_expression_type_query(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, scope, switch_index + 2, header_close, &controlling_type);
 22418:         CTypeKind controlling_kind = C_TYPE_INVALID;
 22419:         u64 value_mask = UINT64_MAX;
 22420:         u64 sign_bit = 0;
 22421:         if (controlling_type_resolved && controlling_type.value < result->type_count)
 22422:         {
 22423:             controlling_kind = c_parse_expression_promoted_kind(c_parse_expression_value_kind(result, controlling_type));
 22424:             u64 size = 0;
 22425:             u32 alignment = 0;
 22426:             controlling_type_resolved = c_parse_expression_integer_kind(controlling_kind) &&
 22427:                                         c_parse_builtin_type_layout(preprocess.target, controlling_kind, &size, &alignment) && size && size <= sizeof(u64);
 22428:             BUSTER_UNUSED(alignment);
 22429:             if (controlling_type_resolved && size < sizeof(u64))
 22430:             {
 22431:                 value_mask = UINT64_MAX >> (64 - (u32)(size * 8));
 22432:             }
 22433:             if (controlling_type_resolved && c_parse_expression_signed_kind(controlling_kind))
 22434:             {
 22435:                 sign_bit = size == sizeof(u64) ? (UINT64_C(1) << 63) : (UINT64_C(1) << ((u32)(size * 8) - 1));
 22436:             }
 22437:         }
 22438:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, controlling_mark);
 22439:         if (!controlling_type_resolved)
 22440:         {
 22441:             if (controlling_type.value < result->type_count)
 22442:             {
 22443:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic,
 22444:                     string_format(result->arena, S8("unsupported C function-body statement or expression near '{S8}'"),
 22445:                                   c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[switch_index + 2])), switch_index, switch_index + 2);
 22446:             }
 22447:         }
 22448:         else
 22449:         {
 22450:             u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22451:             u32 capacity = switch_end - header_close + 1;
 22452:             u64* lows = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, u64, capacity);
 22453:             u64* highs = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, u64, capacity);
 22454:             u32 value_count = 0;
 22455:             bool has_default = false;
 22456:             u32 brace_depth = 0;
 22457:             for (u32 index = header_close + 1; index < switch_end; index += 1)
 22458:             {
 22459:                 CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 22460:                 brace_depth += c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE);
 22461:                 brace_depth -= brace_depth && c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACE);
 22462:                 if (brace_depth > 1 && !value_count && !has_default && c_token_in_well_known_set(preprocess.spelling_base, token,
 22463:                         C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(CASE) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(DEFAULT)))
@@ 22388:22498 @@
 22388:             }
 22389:             index += 1;
 22390:         }
 22391:     }
 22392:     return colon;
 22393: }

 22548:                     {
 22549:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("case range lower bound is not an integer constant expression"), index, index);
 22550:                     }
 22551:                     else
 22552:                     {
 22553:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("unsupported C function-body statement or expression near 'switch'"),
 22554:                                                              switch_index, switch_index);
 22555:                     }
 22556:                     continue;
 22557:                 }
 22558:                 high = low;
 22559:                 if (range_operator != UINT32_MAX)
 22560:                 {
 22561:                     expression_mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22562:                     CParseConstant high_value = c_parse_typed_constant(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, case_scope, range_operator + 1, colon);
 22563:                     bool high_constant = high_value.valid && !high_value.is_float;
 22564:                     high = high_value.integer;
 22565:                     arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, expression_mark);
 22566:                     if (!high_constant)
 22567:                     {
 22568:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("case range upper bound is not an integer constant expression"), index,
 22569:                                                              range_operator + 1);
 22570:                         continue;
 22571:                     }
 22572:                 }
 22573: 
 22574:                 low &= value_mask;
 22575:                 high &= value_mask;
 22576:                 if (range_operator != UINT32_MAX && (low ^ sign_bit) > (high ^ sign_bit))
 22577:                 {
 22578:                     c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("case range is not ordered after conversion to the switch type"), index, index);
 22579:                     continue;
 22580:                 }
 22581:                 for (u32 previous = 0; previous < value_count; previous += 1)
 22582:                 {
 22583:                     bool overlaps = (low ^ sign_bit) <= (highs[previous] ^ sign_bit) && (lows[previous] ^ sign_bit) <= (high ^ sign_bit);
 22584:                     if (overlaps)
 22585:                     {
 22586:                         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("case label overlaps another case label"), index, index);
 22587:                         break;
 22588:                     }
 22589:                 }
 22590:                 lows[value_count] = low;
 22591:                 highs[value_count] = high;
 22592:                 value_count += 1;
 22593:                 index = colon;
 22594:             }
 22595:             arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 22596:         }
 22597:     }
 22598: }

 22600: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_switch_duplicates(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 22601:                                                             CDeclaration const* declaration, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 22602: {
 22603:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 22604:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 22605:     u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22606:     u8* suffix = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, u8, end - start + 1);
 22607:     CParseCandidates switches = c_parse_candidates(result, preprocess, C_PARSE_POPULATION_SWITCH, C_PARSE_POPULATION_NONE, start);
 22608:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&switches, start, end); index < end; index = c_parse_candidates_next(&switches, index + 1, end))
 22609:     {
 22610:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 22611:         if (token.kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER && c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_SWITCH))
 22612:         {
 22613:             c_parse_validate_one_switch(machine, result, preprocess, declaration, index, end, suffix, diagnostic);

 22615:     }

 22619: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_alias_targets(Arena* arena, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess)
 22620: {
 22621:     for (u32 index = 0; index < result->declaration_count; index += 1)
 22622:     {
 22623:         CDeclaration declaration = result->declarations[index];
 22624:         if (declaration.entity.value < result->entity_count &&
 22625:             (declaration.kind == C_DECLARATION_OBJECT || declaration.kind == C_DECLARATION_FUNCTION))
 22626:         {
 22627:             CDeclarationBinding binding = c_declaration_binding(arena, preprocess, declaration);
 22628:             if (binding.alias_target.length)
 22629:             {
 22630:                 CEntityId target = result->scope_count ? c_parse_lookup_entity(result, (CScopeId){.value = 0}, binding.alias_target)
 22631:                                                       : C_ENTITY_ID_INVALID;
 22632:                 String8 message = {0};
 22633:                 if (target.value >= result->entity_count || target.value == declaration.entity.value)
 22634:                 {
 22635:                     message = string_format(arena, S8("alias target '{S8}' is not declared in this translation unit"), binding.alias_target);
 22636:                 }
 22637:                 else if (result->entities[declaration.entity.value].is_definition)
 22638:                 {
 22639:                     message = string_format(arena, S8("'{S8}' is both defined here and aliased to '{S8}'"), declaration.name, binding.alias_target);
 22640:                 }
 22641:                 else if (!result->entities[target.value].is_definition)
@@ 22713:22823 @@
 22713:                 width = c_parse_typed_constant(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, scope,
 22714:                                                member.bit_width_token_start, member.bit_width_token_start + member.bit_width_token_count);
 22715:             }
 22716:             if (width.valid && width.is_float)
 22717:             {
 22718:                 c_parse_diagnostic(result, member.location, C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_BIT_FIELD_WIDTH,
 22719:                                    S8("bit-field width is not an integer constant expression"));
 22720:             }
 22721:             else if (width.valid && !width.integer && member.name.length)
 22722:             {
 22723:                 c_parse_diagnostic(result, member.location, C_DIAGNOSTIC_INVALID_BIT_FIELD_WIDTH,
 22724:                                    string_format(arena, S8("named bit-field '{S8}' has zero width"), member.name));
 22725:             }
 22726:             arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 22727:         }
 22728:     }
 22729: }
 22730: 
 22731: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_builtin_calls(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 22732:                                                        CDeclaration const* declaration, u8 const* skipped, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 22733: {
 22734:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 22735:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 22736:     CParseCandidates calls = c_parse_call_candidates(preprocess);
 22737:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&calls, start, end); index + 1 < end; index = c_parse_candidates_next(&calls, index + 1, end))
 22738:     {
 22739:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 22740:         if (skipped[index - start] || token.kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER ||
 22741:             !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 22742:         {
 22743:             continue;
 22744:         }
 22745:         String8 name = c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, token);
 22746:         u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, index + 1);
 22747:         CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, index);
 22748:         bool sizeof_word = string_equal(name, S8("sizeof")) || c_parse_alignof_word(name);
 22749:         if (sizeof_word && close < end && index + 3 < close &&
 22750:             c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 2], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 22751:         {
 22752:             u32 inner_start = index + 2;
 22753:             u32 inner_end = close;
 22754:             while (inner_start < inner_end && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[inner_start], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS) &&
 22755:                    c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, inner_start) + 1 == inner_end)
 22756:             {
 22757:                 inner_start += 1;
 22758:                 inner_end -= 1;
 22759:             }
 22760:             u32 cursor = inner_start;
 22761:             CTypeId type = c_parse_machineless_base_type(result, preprocess, scope, inner_start, inner_end, &cursor);
 22762:             if (type.value < result->type_count)
 22763:             {
 22764:                 type = c_parse_pointer_chain(result, preprocess, type, &cursor, inner_end);
 22765:                 type = c_parse_array_suffixes(result, preprocess, type, &cursor, inner_end);
 22766:                 if (type.value < result->type_count && cursor == inner_end)
 22767:                 {
 22768:                     c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic,
 22769:                         S8("a parenthesized type name is not an expression; the type form takes exactly one pair of parentheses"), index, index + 2);
 22770:                 }
 22771:             }
 22772:         }
 22773:         CSymbolBuiltin builtin = c_symbol_builtin_from_spelling(name);
 22774:         if (builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_NONE || close >= end)
 22775:         {

 22777:                 !string_equal(name, S8("__builtin_offsetof")) && !c_parse_bfloat16_builtin(name))
 22778:             {

 22784:                 }
 22785:             }
 22786:             continue;
 22787:         }
 22788:         u32 starts[6] = {0};
 22789:         u32 ends[6] = {0};
 22790:         u32 count = 0;
 22791:         for (u32 argument = index + 2; argument < close;)
 22792:         {
 22793:             u32 limit = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, argument, close);
 22794:             if (count < BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(starts))
 22795:             {
 22796:                 starts[count] = argument;
 22797:                 ends[count] = limit;
 22798:             }
 22799:             count += 1;
 22800:             argument = limit + 1;
 22801:         }
 22802:         for (u32 argument = 0; argument < count && argument < BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(starts); argument += 1)
 22803:         {
 22804:             if ((builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_ARG && argument == 1) || string_equal(name, S8("__builtin_types_compatible_p"))) continue;
 22805:             CTypeId type = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 22806:             c_parse_checked_expression_type(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, scope,
 22807:                 starts[argument], ends[argument], &type, diagnostic);
 22808:         }
 22809:         u32 minimum = 0;
 22810:         u32 maximum = UINT32_MAX;
 22811:         switch (builtin)
 22812:         {
 22813:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_ATOMIC: minimum = maximum = c_semantic_atomic_builtin_arity(c_ir_atomic_builtin_spelling(name)); break;
 22814:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_EXPECT: minimum = 2; break;
 22815:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_MEMORY:
 22816:             minimum = maximum = string_starts_with_sequence(name, S8("__builtin___")) ? 4u : 3u;

 22821:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_START_C23:

 22731: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_builtin_calls(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 22732:                                                        CDeclaration const* declaration, u8 const* skipped, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 22733: {
 22734:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 22735:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 22736:     CParseCandidates calls = c_parse_call_candidates(preprocess);
 22737:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&calls, start, end); index + 1 < end; index = c_parse_candidates_next(&calls, index + 1, end))
 22738:     {
 22739:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 22740:         if (skipped[index - start] || token.kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER ||
 22741:             !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 22742:         {
 22743:             continue;
 22744:         }

 22748:         bool sizeof_word = string_equal(name, S8("sizeof")) || c_parse_alignof_word(name);
 22749:         if (sizeof_word && close < end && index + 3 < close &&
 22750:             c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 2], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 22751:         {
 22752:             u32 inner_start = index + 2;
 22753:             u32 inner_end = close;
 22754:             while (inner_start < inner_end && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[inner_start], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS) &&
 22755:                    c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, inner_start) + 1 == inner_end)
 22756:             {
 22757:                 inner_start += 1;
 22758:                 inner_end -= 1;
 22759:             }
 22760:             u32 cursor = inner_start;
 22761:             CTypeId type = c_parse_machineless_base_type(result, preprocess, scope, inner_start, inner_end, &cursor);
 22762:             if (type.value < result->type_count)
 22763:             {
 22764:                 type = c_parse_pointer_chain(result, preprocess, type, &cursor, inner_end);
 22765:                 type = c_parse_array_suffixes(result, preprocess, type, &cursor, inner_end);
 22766:                 if (type.value < result->type_count && cursor == inner_end)
 22767:                 {
 22768:                     c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic,
 22769:                         S8("a parenthesized type name is not an expression; the type form takes exactly one pair of parentheses"), index, index + 2);
 22770:                 }
 22771:             }
 22772:         }
 22773:         CSymbolBuiltin builtin = c_symbol_builtin_from_spelling(name);
 22774:         if (builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_NONE || close >= end)
 22775:         {
 22776:             if (builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_NONE && string_starts_with_sequence(name, S8("__builtin_")) &&
 22777:                 !string_equal(name, S8("__builtin_offsetof")) && !c_parse_bfloat16_builtin(name))
 22778:             {
 22779:                 CEntityId entity = c_parse_lookup_entity_token(result, preprocess.spelling_base, scope, &token);
 22780:                 if (entity.value >= result->entity_count)
 22781:                 {
 22782:                     c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic,
 22783:                         string_format(result->arena, S8("could not lower unbound identifier '{S8}'"), name), index, index);
 22784:                 }
 22785:             }
 22786:             continue;
 22787:         }
 22788:         u32 starts[6] = {0};
 22789:         u32 ends[6] = {0};
 22790:         u32 count = 0;
 22791:         for (u32 argument = index + 2; argument < close;)
 22792:         {
 22793:             u32 limit = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, argument, close);
 22794:             if (count < BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(starts))
 22795:             {
 22796:                 starts[count] = argument;
 22797:                 ends[count] = limit;
 22798:             }
 22799:             count += 1;
 22800:             argument = limit + 1;
 22801:         }
 22802:         for (u32 argument = 0; argument < count && argument < BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(starts); argument += 1)
 22803:         {
 22804:             if ((builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_ARG && argument == 1) || string_equal(name, S8("__builtin_types_compatible_p"))) continue;
 22805:             CTypeId type = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 22806:             c_parse_checked_expression_type(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, scope,
 22807:                 starts[argument], ends[argument], &type, diagnostic);
 22808:         }
 22809:         u32 minimum = 0;
 22810:         u32 maximum = UINT32_MAX;
 22811:         switch (builtin)
 22812:         {
 22813:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_ATOMIC: minimum = maximum = c_semantic_atomic_builtin_arity(c_ir_atomic_builtin_spelling(name)); break;
 22814:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_EXPECT: minimum = 2; break;
 22815:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_MEMORY:
 22816:             minimum = maximum = string_starts_with_sequence(name, S8("__builtin___")) ? 4u : 3u;
 22817:             break;
 22818:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_COMPLEX:
 22819:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_ARG:
 22820:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_START:

 22823:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_END:
 22824:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_ALLOCA:

 22826:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_COUNT_LEADING_ZEROS:
 22827:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_COUNT_TRAILING_ZEROS:
 22828:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_FIND_FIRST_SET:

 22830:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_DEBUGTRAP:
 22831:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_SPIN_PAUSE:
 22832:         case C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_UNREACHABLE: maximum = 0; break;
 22833:         default: break;
 22834:         }
 22835:         String8 message = count < minimum || count > maximum ? S8("could not prepare C calls") : (String8){0};
 22836:         u32 location = close;
 22837:         if (builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_FIND_FIRST_SET && !message.length)
@@ 23019:23129 @@
 23019:             location = index;
 23020:         }
 23021:         else if (builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_ARG || builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_START || builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_START_C23 ||
 23022:                  builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_COPY || builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_END)
 23023:         {
 23024:             bool write = builtin != C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_ARG && builtin != C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_END;
 23025:             bool valid = count == (builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_END ? 1u : 2u);
 23026:             u32 operands = builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_COPY ? 2u : 1u;
 23027:             for (u32 argument = 0; valid && argument < operands; argument += 1)
 23028:             {
 23029:                 CTypeId type = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 23030:                 u32 operand_start = starts[argument];
 23031:                 if (write && !argument && string_equal(name, S8("__va_start")) &&
 23032:                     c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[operand_start], C_PUNCTUATOR_AMPERSAND))
 23033:                 {
 23034:                     operand_start += 1;
 23035:                 }
 23036:                 valid = c_parse_expression_type_query(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, scope, operand_start, ends[argument], &type);
 23037:                 if (valid && (!write || argument) && type.value < result->type_count && result->types[type.value].kind == C_TYPE_POINTER)
 23038:                 {
 23039:                     type = result->types[type.value].element_type;
 23040:                 }
 23041:                 valid &= type.value < result->type_count && result->types[type.value].kind == C_TYPE_VA_LIST &&
 23042:                          (!(write && !argument) || (!result->types[type.value].is_const && !result->types[type.value].is_atomic));
 23043:             }
 23044:             if (builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_START || builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_START_C23)
 23045:             {
 23046:                 valid &= declaration->type.value < result->type_count && result->types[declaration->type.value].is_variadic;
 23047:             }

 23051:                           builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_ARG ? S8("va_arg requires a va_list operand") : S8("va_end requires a va_list operand");
 23052:             }
 23053:             if (valid && builtin == C_SYMBOL_BUILTIN_VA_ARG)
 23054:             {
 23055:                 u32 cursor = starts[1];
 23056:                 CTypeId type = c_parse_type_name_specifiers(machine, result, preprocess, scope, cursor, ends[1], &cursor);
 23057:                 if (type.value < result->type_count) type = c_parse_pointer_chain(result, preprocess, type, &cursor, ends[1]);
 23058:                 if (c_parse_type_contains_wide_float(machine, result, preprocess.target, type))
 23059:                 {
 23060:                     u64 size = 0;
 23061:                     u32 alignment = 0;
 23062:                     CType value = result->types[type.value];
 23063:                     bool supported = preprocess.target.cpu_arch == CPU_ARCH_X86_64 &&
 23064:                         ir_abi_convention_for_target(preprocess.target) == IR_ABI_CONVENTION_SYSTEMV_X86_64 &&
 23065:                         target_data_layout(preprocess.target).long_double_type.bit_width == 80 && !value.is_atomic &&
 23066:                         value.kind != C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX &&
 23067:                         c_parse_type_layout(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, type, &size, &alignment) && size <= 16;
 23068:                     if (!supported) message = S8("C IR lowering does not yet support wide floating-point va_arg");
 23069:                 }
 23070:             }
 23071:             location = index;
 23072:         }
 23073:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 23074:         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, message, index, location);

 23077: 
 23078: // Whether the `&&` at `index`, whose previous token is a `)`, closes a cast
 23079: // to a type name the scope resolves, so the operator spells a label address
 23080: // rather than the conjunction the proven prefix test already rejected. A
 23081: // parenthesized type name that is a sizeof/alignof operand is that operator's
 23082: // operand, not a cast: `sizeof(int) && a` is a conjunction, as the proven
 23083: // test already says of it.
 23084: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_label_address_cast_at(CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CScopeId scope, u32 start, u32 index)
 23085: {
 23086:     bool label = false;
 23087:     u32 depth = 1;
 23088:     u32 open = index - 1;
 23089:     while (open > start && depth)
 23090:     {
 23091:         open -= 1;
 23092:         depth += c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[open], C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS);
 23093:         depth -= c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[open], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS);
 23094:     }
 23095:     bool operand = !depth && open > start && preprocess.tokens[open - 1].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER &&
 23096:                    (c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[open - 1], C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_SIZEOF) ||
 23097:                     c_parse_alignof_word(c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[open - 1])));
 23098:     if (!depth && !operand)
 23099:     {
 23100:         u32 cursor = open + 1;
 23101:         CTypeId type = c_parse_machineless_base_type(result, preprocess, scope, cursor, index - 1, &cursor);
 23102:         if (type.value < result->type_count)
 23103:         {
 23104:             type = c_parse_pointer_chain(result, preprocess, type, &cursor, index - 1);
 23105:             label = type.value < result->type_count && cursor == index - 1;
 23106:         }
 23107:     }
 23108:     return label;
 23109: }

 23111: // Source label facts are compact entity flags. Aggregate storage keeps the
 23112: // union of its possible labels; canonical control-flow validation still owns
 23113: // the exact target edges and subobject provenance of generated branches.
 23114: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_label_expression(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CScopeId scope,
 23115:                                                   u8 const* labels, u32 start, u32 end)
 23116: {
 23117:     bool label = false;
 23118:     for (u32 index = start; !label && index < end; index += 1)
 23119:     {
 23120:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 23121:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_AMPERSAND_AMPERSAND) && index + 1 < end)
 23122:         {
 23123:             label = c_parse_label_address_prefix_proven(&preprocess, start, index) ||
 23124:                     (index > start && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS) &&
 23125:                      c_parse_label_address_cast_at(result, preprocess, scope, start, index));
 23126:         }
 23127:         else if (token.kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER)
 23128:         {
 23129:             CEntityId entity = c_parse_lookup_entity_token(result, preprocess.spelling_base, scope, &token);
@@ 23189:23299 @@
 23189:     for (u32 index = start; named_label && conjunction && !needed && index + 1 < end; index += 1)
 23190:     {
 23191:         if ((!skipped || !skipped[index - start]) && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index], C_PUNCTUATOR_AMPERSAND_AMPERSAND))
 23192:         {
 23193:             needed = c_parse_label_address_prefix_proven(&preprocess, start, index) ||
 23194:                      (index > start && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS) &&
 23195:                       c_parse_label_address_cast_at(result, preprocess, c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, index), start, index));
 23196:         }
 23197:     }
 23198:     return needed;
 23199: }

 23201: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_label_values(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 23202:                                                       CDeclaration const* declaration, u8 const* skipped, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 23203: {
 23204:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 23205:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 23206:     if (c_parse_label_values_needed(result, preprocess, declaration, skipped))
 23207:     {
 23208:         u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 23209:         u8* labels = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, u8, result->entity_count);
 23210:         memset(labels, 0, result->entity_count);
 23211:         u32* openers = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, u32, end - start);
 23212:         memset(openers, 0xff, sizeof(*openers) * (end - start));
 23213:         for (u32 index = start; index < end; index += 1)
 23214:         {
 23215:             CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 23216:             if (token.kind == C_TOKEN_PUNCTUATOR && c_punctuator_in_set(token.punctuator, C_PUNCTUATOR_SET_DELIMITER_OPEN))
 23217:             {
 23218:                 u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, index);
 23219:                 if (close < end) openers[close - start] = index;
 23220:             }
 23221:         }
 23222:         for (u32 index = start; index + 1 < end; index += 1)
 23223:         {
 23224:             if (skipped[index - start])
 23225:             {
 23226:                 continue;
 23227:             }
 23228:             CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 23229:             if (token.kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET))
 23230:             {
 23231:                 CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, index);
 23232:                 CEntityId entity = c_parse_lookup_entity_token(result, preprocess.spelling_base, scope, &token);
 23233:                 if (entity.value < result->entity_count && labels[entity.value])
 23234:                 {
 23235:                     CTypeId type = result->entities[entity.value].type;
 23236:                     CTypeId element = type.value < result->type_count ? result->types[type.value].element_type : C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 23237:                     CTypeKind kind = element.value < result->type_count ? result->types[element.value].kind : C_TYPE_INVALID;
 23238:                     if (kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || kind == C_TYPE_UNION || kind == C_TYPE_ARRAY)
 23239:                     {
 23240:                         u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, index + 1);
 23241:                         CParseConstant subscript = close < end ? c_parse_typed_constant(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, scope, index + 2, close)
 23242:                                                               : (CParseConstant){0};
 23243:                         if (!subscript.valid || subscript.is_float)
 23244:                             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("dynamic indexing of label-containing aggregate elements is unsupported"), index, index + 1);
 23245:                     }
 23246:                 }
 23247:             }
 23248:             bool assignment = c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_ASSIGN);
 23249:             bool returning = c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_RETURN);
 23250:             bool computed = c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_GOTO) &&
 23251:                             c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_STAR);
 23252:             bool call = token.kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS);
 23253:             if (!assignment && !returning && !computed && !call)
 23254:             {
 23255:                 continue;
 23256:             }
 23257:             CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, index);
 23258:             u32 value_start = index + 1 + (computed ? 1u : 0u);
 23259:             u32 value_end = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, value_start, end);
 23260:             bool label = c_parse_label_expression(machine, result, preprocess, scope, labels, value_start, value_end);
 23261:             String8 message = {0};
 23262:             u32 location = value_end;
 23263:             if (assignment)
 23264:             {
 23265:                 CEntityId destination = C_ENTITY_ID_INVALID;
 23266:                 u32 place = c_parse_constraint_postfix_start(result, preprocess, scope, start, index, openers);
 23267:                 if (place < index && preprocess.tokens[place].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER)
 23268:                 {
 23269:                     destination = c_parse_lookup_entity_token(result, preprocess.spelling_base, scope, &preprocess.tokens[place]);
 23270:                 }
 23271:                 if (destination.value < result->entity_count)
 23272:                 {
 23273:                     CEntity entity = result->entities[destination.value];
 23274:                     bool initializer = index >= entity.declaration_token_start && index < entity.declaration_token_start + entity.declaration_token_count;
 23275:                     if (label && (entity.kind == C_ENTITY_OBJECT || (entity.is_static_storage && !initializer)))
 23276:                     {
 23277:                         message = S8("a label address may not be stored in static storage");
 23278:                         location = value_end > value_start ? value_end - 1 : value_start;
 23279:                     }
 23280:                     CTypeKind kind = entity.type.value < result->type_count ? result->types[entity.type.value].kind : C_TYPE_INVALID;
 23281:                     bool aggregate = kind == C_TYPE_ARRAY || kind == C_TYPE_STRUCT || kind == C_TYPE_UNION;
 23282:                     labels[destination.value] = (u8)(label || (aggregate && labels[destination.value]));
 23283:                 }
 23284:             }
 23285:             else if (returning && label)
 23286:             {
 23287:                 message = S8("a label address may not escape through a function return");
 23288:                 location = value_end > value_start ? value_end - 1 : value_start;
 23289:             }
 23290:             else if (computed)
 23291:             {
 23292:                 if (!label)
 23293:                 {
 23294:                     message = S8("computed goto requires a function-local void pointer label value");
 23295:                     location = value_start;
 23296:                 }
 23297:                 else

 23345: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL String8 c_parse_asm_constraint_shape(String8 text, bool output)
 23346: {
 23347:     String8 message = {0};
 23348:     bool modifier = text.length == 2 || (text.length == 3 && text.pointer[1] == '&');
 23349:     if (output && (!modifier || (text.pointer[0] != '=' && text.pointer[0] != '+')))
 23350:     {
 23351:         message = S8("malformed asm output constraint");
 23352:     }
 23353:     else
 23354:     {
 23355:         u64 prefix = output ? text.length - 1 : 0;
 23356:         String8 classes = output ? S8("abcdSDrmxt") : S8("abcdSDrmxtuX");
 23357:         bool admitted = false;
 23358:         if (text.length == prefix + 1)
 23359:         {
 23360:             for (u64 index = 0; index < classes.length; index += 1) admitted |= text.pointer[prefix] == classes.pointer[index];
 23361:         }
 23362:         if (!admitted && output)
 23363:         {
 23364:             message = S8("unsupported asm output constraint");
 23365:         }
 23366:         else if (!admitted)
 23367:         {
 23368:             bool decimal = text.length != 0;
 23369:             for (u64 index = 0; index < text.length; index += 1) decimal &= text.pointer[index] >= '0' && text.pointer[index] <= '9';
 23370:             bool named = text.length >= 3 && text.pointer[0] == '[' && text.pointer[text.length - 1] == ']';
 23371:             for (u64 index = 1; named && index + 1 < text.length; index += 1) named &= text.pointer[index] != '[' && text.pointer[index] != ']';
 23372:             if (!decimal && !named) message = text.length == 1 ? S8("malformed asm matching constraint") : S8("malformed asm input constraint");
 23373:         }
 23374:     }
 23375:     return message;
 23376: }

 23380: {
 23381:     u64 result = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_COUNT;
 23382:     switch (name)
 23383:     {
 23384:     case 'a': result = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_A; break;
 23385:     case 'b': result = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_B; break;
 23386:     case 'c': result = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_C; break;
@@ 23686:23796 @@
 23686:                     }
 23687:                 }
 23688:             }
 23689:         }
 23690:         if (!message.length && c_semantic_asm_fixed_operands_conflict(constraints, operand_count))
 23691:             message = S8("asm fixed-register operands conflict without a supported matching constraint");
 23692:         if (!message.length) message = c_semantic_asm_x87_operands_message(constraints, operand_count, stack_clobber);
 23693:         if (!message.length && separator_count <= 4)
 23694:         {
 23695:             CIrDecodedString decoded = {0};
 23696:             bool valid = c_ir_decode_string_literal_range_for_target(machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, preprocess.target, open + 1, template_end, &decoded);
 23697:             String8 assembly = {.pointer = (char8*)decoded.bytes.pointer, .length = decoded.element_count};
 23698:             if (valid) message = c_semantic_asm_special_operands_message(preprocess.target, assembly, constraints, operands,
 23699:                 operand_count, output_count, rbx_clobber);
 23700:             for (u64 offset = 0; valid && !message.length && offset + 1 < assembly.length; offset += 1)
 23701:             {
 23702:                 if (assembly.pointer[offset] != '%') continue;
 23703:                 if (assembly.pointer[offset + 1] == '%')
 23704:                 {
 23705:                     offset += 1;
 23706:                     continue;
 23707:                 }
 23708:                 bool label = assembly.pointer[offset + 1] == 'l';
 23709:                 u64 begin = offset + (label ? 2u : 1u);
 23710:                 if (begin >= assembly.length || assembly.pointer[begin] != '[') continue;
 23711:                 u64 limit = begin + 1;
 23712:                 while (limit < assembly.length && assembly.pointer[limit] != ']') limit += 1;
 23713:                 if (limit >= assembly.length) continue;
 23714:                 String8 operand = {.pointer = assembly.pointer + begin + 1, .length = limit - begin - 1};
 23715:                 bool found = false;
 23716:                 u32 names_start = label ? (separator_count == 4 ? separators[3] + 1 : close) : separators[0] + 1;
 23717:                 u32 names_end = label ? close : separator_count >= 3 ? separators[2] : close;
 23718:                 for (u32 cursor = names_start; !found && cursor < names_end; cursor += 1)
 23719:                 {
 23720:                     CToken current = preprocess.tokens[cursor];
 23721:                     if (c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 23722:                     {
 23723:                         u32 nested = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, cursor);
 23724:                         cursor = nested < names_end ? nested : names_end;
 23725:                     }
 23726:                     else if (current.kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER && (label || (cursor > names_start &&
 23727:                              c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[cursor - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET))))
 23728:                     {
 23729:                         found = string_equal(operand, c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, current));
 23730:                     }
 23731:                 }
 23732:                 if (!found)
 23733:                 {
 23734:                     message = string_format(result->arena, S8("unknown asm {S8} name '{S8}'"), label ? S8("label") : S8("operand"), operand);
 23735:                     location = close;
 23736:                 }
 23737:                 offset = limit;
 23738:             }
 23739:         }
 23740:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 23741:         c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, message, index, location);
 23742:         index = close;
 23743:     }
 23744: }

 23746: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_body_delimiters_valid(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 23747:                                                        CDeclaration const* declaration)
 23748: {
 23749:     bool valid = true;
 23750:     if (result->position_index && result->position_index->delimiter_mismatch_count)
 23751:     {
 23752:         u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 23753:         u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 23754:         u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 23755:         CPunctuator* stack = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, CPunctuator, end - start);
 23756:         u32 count = 0;
 23757:         for (u32 index = start; valid && index < end; index += 1)
 23758:         {
 23759:             CPunctuator token = (CPunctuator)preprocess.tokens[index].punctuator;
 23760:             if (token == C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS || token == C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET || token == C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE)
 23761:                 stack[count++] = token;
 23762:             CPunctuator expected = token == C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_PARENTHESIS ? C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS
 23763:                                    : token == C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACKET ? C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET
 23764:                                    : token == C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACE ? C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE : C_PUNCTUATOR_NONE;
 23765:             if (expected != C_PUNCTUATOR_NONE)
 23766:             {
 23767:                 valid = count && stack[count - 1] == expected;
 23768:                 if (valid) count -= 1;
 23769:             }
 23770:         }
 23771:         valid &= count == 0;
 23772:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 23773:     }
 23774:     return valid;
 23775: }

 23777: typedef struct CParseControlRange CParseControlRange;
 23778: struct CParseControlRange
 23779: {
 23780:     u32 end;
 23781:     bool loop;
 23782: };
 23783: 
 23784: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_control_statements(CTypeParseMachine* machine, CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess,
 23785:                                                              CDeclaration const* declaration, u8 const* skipped, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 23786: {
 23787:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 23788:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 23789:     u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 23790:     CParseControlRange* ranges = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, CParseControlRange, end - start);
 23791:     u8* suffix = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, u8, end - start + 1);
 23792:     u32 count = 0;
 23793:     u32 loops = 0;
 23794:     u32 switches = 0;
 23795:     // Ranges close in stack order, so popping them at the next candidate
 23796:     // leaves the same loop/switch depth every candidate saw before.
 23797:     CParseCandidates candidates = c_parse_candidates(result, preprocess, C_PARSE_POPULATION_CONTROL_KEYWORDS, C_PARSE_POPULATION_BRACE_IDENTIFIERS, start);
 23798:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&candidates, start, end); index < end; index = c_parse_candidates_next(&candidates, index + 1, end))
 23799:     {
 23800:         while (count && ranges[count - 1].end <= index)
 23801:         {
 23802:             count -= 1;
 23803:             loops -= ranges[count].loop;
 23804:             switches -= !ranges[count].loop;
 23805:         }
 23806:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 23807:         if (skipped[index - start] || token.kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER) continue;
 23808:         if (index + 1 < end && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE) &&
 23809:             (index == start || c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE) ||
 23810:              c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACE) ||
 23811:              c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON)))
 23812:         {
 23813:             CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, index);
 23814:             CEntityId entity = c_parse_lookup_entity_token(result, preprocess.spelling_base, scope, &token);
 23815:             if (entity.value < result->entity_count && result->entities[entity.value].kind == C_ENTITY_TYPEDEF)
 23816:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("unsupported C function-body statement or expression"), index, index);
 23817:         }
 23818:         bool loop = c_token_in_well_known_set(preprocess.spelling_base, token,
 23819:                         C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(FOR) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(WHILE) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(DO));
 23820:         bool selection = c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_SWITCH);
 23821:         if (loop || selection)
 23822:         {
 23823:             u32 body = index + 1;
 23824:             if (!c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_DO) && body < end &&
 23825:                 c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[body], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 23826:             {
 23827:                 body = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, body) + 1;
 23828:             }
 23829:             u32 limit = body < end ? c_parse_statement_end(preprocess, body, end, suffix, end - start + 1) : UINT32_MAX;
 23830:             if (limit <= end)
 23831:             {
 23832:                 ranges[count++] = (CParseControlRange){.end = limit, .loop = loop};
 23833:                 loops += loop;
 23834:                 switches += !loop;
 23835:             }
 23836:         }
 23837:         else if ((c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BREAK) && !loops && !switches) ||
 23838:                  (c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_CONTINUE) && !loops))
 23839:         {
 23840:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic,
 23841:                 string_format(result->arena, S8("unsupported C function-body statement or expression near '{S8}'"),
 23842:                               c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, token)), index, index);
 23843:         }
 23844:         else if (!switches && !(index > start && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA)) &&
 23845:                  c_token_in_well_known_set(preprocess.spelling_base, token,
 23846:                      C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(CASE) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(DEFAULT)))
 23847:         {
 23848:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic,
 23849:                 string_format(result->arena, S8("could not lower unbound identifier '{S8}'"), c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, token)), index, index);
 23850:         }
 23851:     }
 23852:     arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 23853: }

 23859:     CDeclaration const* declaration, u32 cursor, u32 end, CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 23860: {
 23861:     while (cursor < end)
 23862:     {
 23863:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[cursor];
 23864:         if (c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE) || c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACE) ||
 23865:             c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON) || c_token_in_well_known_set(preprocess.spelling_base, token,

 23793:     u32 loops = 0;
 23794:     u32 switches = 0;
 23795:     // Ranges close in stack order, so popping them at the next candidate
 23796:     // leaves the same loop/switch depth every candidate saw before.
 23797:     CParseCandidates candidates = c_parse_candidates(result, preprocess, C_PARSE_POPULATION_CONTROL_KEYWORDS, C_PARSE_POPULATION_BRACE_IDENTIFIERS, start);
 23798:     for (u32 index = c_parse_candidates_next(&candidates, start, end); index < end; index = c_parse_candidates_next(&candidates, index + 1, end))
 23799:     {
 23800:         while (count && ranges[count - 1].end <= index)
 23801:         {
 23802:             count -= 1;
 23803:             loops -= ranges[count].loop;
 23804:             switches -= !ranges[count].loop;
 23805:         }
 23806:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[index];
 23807:         if (skipped[index - start] || token.kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER) continue;
 23808:         if (index + 1 < end && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE) &&
 23809:             (index == start || c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE) ||
 23810:              c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_RIGHT_BRACE) ||
 23811:              c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_SEMICOLON)))
 23812:         {
 23813:             CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, index);
 23814:             CEntityId entity = c_parse_lookup_entity_token(result, preprocess.spelling_base, scope, &token);
 23815:             if (entity.value < result->entity_count && result->entities[entity.value].kind == C_ENTITY_TYPEDEF)
 23816:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("unsupported C function-body statement or expression"), index, index);
 23817:         }
 23818:         bool loop = c_token_in_well_known_set(preprocess.spelling_base, token,
 23819:                         C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(FOR) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(WHILE) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(DO));
 23820:         bool selection = c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_SWITCH);
 23821:         if (loop || selection)
 23822:         {

 23824:             if (!c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_DO) && body < end &&

 23830:             if (limit <= end)
 23831:             {
 23832:                 ranges[count++] = (CParseControlRange){.end = limit, .loop = loop};
 23833:                 loops += loop;
 23834:                 switches += !loop;
 23835:             }
 23836:         }
 23837:         else if ((c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BREAK) && !loops && !switches) ||
 23838:                  (c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_CONTINUE) && !loops))
 23839:         {
 23840:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic,
 23841:                 string_format(result->arena, S8("unsupported C function-body statement or expression near '{S8}'"),
 23842:                               c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, token)), index, index);
 23843:         }
 23844:         else if (!switches && !(index > start && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index - 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA)) &&
 23845:                  c_token_in_well_known_set(preprocess.spelling_base, token,
 23846:                      C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(CASE) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(DEFAULT)))
 23847:         {
 23848:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic,
 23849:                 string_format(result->arena, S8("could not lower unbound identifier '{S8}'"), c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, token)), index, index);
 23850:         }
 23851:     }
 23852:     arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);

 23866:                 C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(ELSE) | C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_BIT(DO)))

 23869:             continue;

 23875:         {

 23884:                     limit = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, limit + 1, close);

 23888:                     result, scope, part, limit, &type, diagnostic);

 23893:                     CTypeKind kind = result->types[type.value].kind;

 23899:                 part = limit + 1;
 23900:                 part_index += 1;
 23901:             }
 23902:             cursor = close < end ? close + 1 : end;
 23903:             continue;
 23904:         }
 23905:         if (token.kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER && c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_CASE))

 23908:             u32 questions = 0;

 23910:             {

 23913:                 {
 23914:                     u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, cursor);
 23915:                     cursor = close < end ? close : end - 1;
 23916:                 }
 23917:                 else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_QUESTION)) questions += 1;
 23918:                 else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_COLON))
 23919:                 {

 22184: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL void c_parse_validate_generic_duplicates(CTypeParseMachine* machine, Arena* arena, CParseResult* result,
 22185:                                                              CPreprocessResult preprocess, CDeclaration const* declaration, u8* skipped,
 22186:                                                              CParseLoweringConstraintDiagnostic* diagnostic)
 22187: {
 22188:     u32 start = declaration->body_start;
 22189:     u32 end = BUSTER_MIN((u32)preprocess.token_count, start + declaration->body_token_count);
 22190:     for (u32 generic = start; generic + 2 < end; generic += 1)
 22191:     {
 22192:         CToken token = preprocess.tokens[generic];
 22193:         if ((skipped && skipped[generic - start]) || token.kind != C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER || !string_equal(c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, token), S8("_Generic")) ||
 22194:             !c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[generic + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 22195:         {
 22196:             continue;
 22197:         }
 22198:         u32 close = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, generic + 1);
 22199:         if (close >= end)
 22200:         {
 22201:             continue;
 22202:         }
 22203:         u32 controller_end = UINT32_MAX;
 22204:         for (u32 index = generic + 2; index < close && controller_end == UINT32_MAX; index += 1)
 22205:         {
 22206:             CToken current = preprocess.tokens[index];
 22207:             if (c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS) ||
 22208:                 c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET) || c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE))
 22209:             {
 22210:                 u32 nested = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, index);
 22211:                 index = nested < close ? nested : close;
 22212:             }
 22213:             else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA))
 22214:             {
 22215:                 controller_end = index;
 22216:             }
 22217:         }
 22218:         if (controller_end == UINT32_MAX)
 22219:         {
 22220:             continue;
 22221:         }
 22222: 
 22223:         CScopeId generic_scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, generic);
 22224:         u64 scratch_mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22225:         CTypeId controller_type = C_TYPE_ID_INVALID;
 22226:         bool controller_resolved = c_parse_expression_type_query(machine, machine->scratch_arena, preprocess, result, generic_scope, generic + 2,
 22227:                                                                  controller_end, &controller_type);
 22228:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, scratch_mark);
 22229:         if (controller_resolved)
 22230:         {
 22231:             controller_type = c_parse_auto_decay_type(result, controller_type);
 22232:         }
 22233: 
 22234:         u64 mark = machine->scratch_arena->position;
 22235:         CTypeId* association_types = arena_allocate(machine->scratch_arena, CTypeId, close - controller_end + 1);
 22236:         u32 association_type_count = 0;
 22237:         u32 match_count = 0;
 22238:         u32 default_count = 0;
 22239:         bool generic_valid = true;
 22240:         u32 association_start = controller_end + 1;
 22241:         while (association_start < close && generic_valid)
 22242:         {
 22243:             u32 colon = UINT32_MAX;
 22244:             u32 association_end = close;
 22245:             for (u32 index = association_start; index < close; index += 1)
 22246:             {
 22247:                 CToken current = preprocess.tokens[index];
 22248:                 if (c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS) ||
 22249:                     c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACKET) || c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_BRACE))
 22250:                 {
 22251:                     u32 nested = c_parse_matching_delimiter_indexed(result, preprocess, index);
 22252:                     index = nested < close ? nested : close;
 22253:                 }
 22254:                 else if (colon == UINT32_MAX && c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_COLON))
 22255:                 {
 22256:                     colon = index;
 22257:                 }
 22258:                 else if (c_token_is_punctuator(&current, C_PUNCTUATOR_COMMA))
 22259:                 {
 22260:                     association_end = index;
 22261:                     break;
 22262:                 }
 22263:             }
 22264:             if (colon == UINT32_MAX || colon == association_start || colon + 1 >= association_end)
 22265:             {
 22266:                 c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("expected 'type-name: expression' or 'default: expression' in _Generic association"),
 22267:                                                      association_start, association_start);
 22268:                 generic_valid = false;
 22269:                 continue;
 22270:             }
 22271:             bool is_default = colon == association_start + 1 && preprocess.tokens[association_start].kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER &&
 22272:                               string_equal(c_token_spelling(preprocess.spelling_base, preprocess.tokens[association_start]), S8("default"));
 22273:             if (is_default)
 22274:             {
 22275:                 default_count += 1;
 22276:                 if (default_count > 1)
 22277:                 {
 22278:                     c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("_Generic selection has more than one default association"),
 22279:                                                          association_start, association_start);
 22280:                     generic_valid = false;
 22281:                 }
 22282:             }
 22283:             else
 22284:             {
 22285:                 CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, association_start);
 22286:                 u32 type_index = association_start;
 22287:                 CTypeId type = c_parse_machineless_base_type(result, preprocess, scope, association_start, colon, &type_index);
 22288:                 if (type.value < result->type_count)
 22289:                 {
 22290:                     type = c_parse_pointer_chain(result, preprocess, type, &type_index, colon);
 22291:                     type = c_parse_array_suffixes(result, preprocess, type, &type_index, colon);
 22292:                     if (type_index < colon && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[type_index], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS))
 22293:                     {
@@ 22265:22270 @@
 22265:             {

 22267:                                                      association_start, association_start);
 22268:                 generic_valid = false;
 22269:                 continue;
 22270:             }
@@ 22277:22281 @@

 23078: // Whether the `&&` at `index`, whose previous token is a `)`, closes a cast
 23079: // to a type name the scope resolves, so the operator spells a label address
 23080: // rather than the conjunction the proven prefix test already rejected. A
 23081: // parenthesized type name that is a sizeof/alignof operand is that operator's
 23082: // operand, not a cast: `sizeof(int) && a` is a conjunction, as the proven
 23083: // test already says of it.
 23084: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL bool c_parse_label_address_cast_at(CParseResult* result, CPreprocessResult preprocess, CScopeId scope, u32 start, u32 index)
 23085: {
 23086:     bool label = false;
 23087:     u32 depth = 1;
 23088:     u32 open = index - 1;
 23089:     while (open > start && depth)
 23090:     {
@@ 23243:23276 @@
 23243:                         if (!subscript.valid || subscript.is_float)
 23244:                             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, S8("dynamic indexing of label-containing aggregate elements is unsupported"), index, index + 1);
 23245:                     }
 23246:                 }
 23247:             }
 23248:             bool assignment = c_token_is_punctuator(&token, C_PUNCTUATOR_ASSIGN);
 23249:             bool returning = c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_RETURN);
 23250:             bool computed = c_token_is_well_known(preprocess.spelling_base, token, C_SYMBOL_WELL_KNOWN_GOTO) &&
 23251:                             c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_STAR);
 23252:             bool call = token.kind == C_TOKEN_IDENTIFIER && c_token_is_punctuator(&preprocess.tokens[index + 1], C_PUNCTUATOR_LEFT_PARENTHESIS);
 23253:             if (!assignment && !returning && !computed && !call)
 23254:             {
 23255:                 continue;
 23256:             }
 23257:             CScopeId scope = c_parse_scope_for_token(result, declaration->scope, index);
 23258:             u32 value_start = index + 1 + (computed ? 1u : 0u);
 23259:             u32 value_end = c_parse_constraint_expression_end(result, preprocess, value_start, end);
 23260:             bool label = c_parse_label_expression(machine, result, preprocess, scope, labels, value_start, value_end);
 23261:             String8 message = {0};
 23262:             u32 location = value_end;
 23263:             if (assignment)
 23264:             {
 23265:                 CEntityId destination = C_ENTITY_ID_INVALID;
 23266:                 u32 place = c_parse_constraint_postfix_start(result, preprocess, scope, start, index, openers);

 23268:                 {

 23270:                 }

 23272:                 {

 23275:                     if (label && (entity.kind == C_ENTITY_OBJECT || (entity.is_static_storage && !initializer)))
 23276:                     {
@@ 23331:23359 @@
 23331:             c_parse_lowering_constraint_consider(diagnostic, message, index, location);
 23332:         }
 23333:         arena_set_position(machine->scratch_arena, mark);
 23334:     }
 23335:

 23345: BUSTER_C_INTERNAL String8 c_parse_asm_constraint_shape(String8 text, bool output)
 23346: {
 23347:     String8 message = {0};
 23348:     bool modifier = text.length == 2 || (text.length == 3 && text.pointer[1] == '&');
 23349:     if (output && (!modifier || (text.pointer[0] != '=' && text.pointer[0] != '+')))
 23350:     {
 23351:         message = S8("malformed asm output constraint");
 23352:     }
 23353:     else
 23354:     {
 23355:         u64 prefix = output ? text.length - 1 : 0;
 23356:         String8 classes = output ? S8("abcdSDrmxt") : S8("abcdSDrmxtuX");
 23357:         bool admitted = false;
 23358:         if (text.length == prefix + 1)
 23359:         {
@@ 23741:23751 @@

   728:            : kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX      ? C_TYPE_DOUBLE
   729:            : kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX ? C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE
   730:                                                 : C_TYPE_INVALID;
   731: }
   732: 
   733: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE CTypeKind c_type_kind_complex_of(CTypeKind kind)
   734: {
   735:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX
   736:            : kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX
   737:            : kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE      ? C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX
   738:            : kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE ? C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX
   739:                                         : C_TYPE_INVALID;
   740: }

   758: struct CType
   759: {
   760:     String8 tag;

   762:     // definitions of one tag in sibling functions stay two types -- clang's
   763:     // xmmintrin.h defines `struct __mm_storeh_pi_struct` inside two
   764:     // intrinsics -- and a reference resolves to the innermost visible one.

   789:     // argument of any member's type, passed as that member's own bits.

@@ 837:907 @@
   837: 
   838: // The GNU attributes a struct or union definition carries on the definition
   839: // itself, either before the tag (`struct __attribute__((packed)) P { ... }`)
   840: // or after the closing brace (`struct P { ... } __attribute__((packed));`).
   841: // They live in a side table rather than in CType because the population is a
   842: // handful of aggregates in a translation unit while the type table is tens of
   843: // thousands of entries: a flag word per type would grow every one of them.
   844: // Both layout engines -- the sizeof folding in c_parse.c and the IR layout in
   845: // c_gen.c -- read this table, and they must agree or a folded sizeof
   846: // contradicts the object it sizes.
   847: typedef struct CAggregateAttributes CAggregateAttributes;
   848: struct CAggregateAttributes
   849: {
   850:     u32 type_index;
   851:     // Range into CParseResult.alignments, as CMember/CDeclaration name theirs.
   852:     u32 alignment_start;
   853:     u32 alignment_count;
   854:     bool is_packed;
   855:     u8 reserved[3];
   856: };
   857: 
   858: // The alignment a declarator wrote on the *type* it declares rather than on an
   859: // object it declares: `typedef int cache_line __attribute__((aligned(64)))`.
   860: // GNU `aligned` there sets the alignment of the type, so unlike every other
   861: // alignment record in this file it *replaces* the natural alignment instead of
   862: // raising it, and it is the one place the attribute lowers one without
   863: // `packed` -- `typedef int pair __attribute__((aligned(2)))` is two-byte
   864: // aligned in both GCC and Clang. The record keys on the alias copy the
   865: // declarator made, never on the type it aliases, so `int` keeps its own
   866: // alignment -- and on every qualified copy of that alias, because a qualifier
   867: // cannot take an alignment away and the qualified copy points past the alias
   868: // at the type it strips to (#714). It lives in a side table for the reason
   869: // CParseResult.noreturn_function_types does: the population is a handful of
   870: // typedefs per translation unit against a type table of tens of thousands of
   871: // entries, so a field on CType would cost every one of them.
   872: typedef struct CTypeAlignment CTypeAlignment;
   873: struct CTypeAlignment
   874: {
   875:     u32 type_index;
   876:     // Range into CParseResult.alignments, as CMember/CDeclaration name theirs.
   877:     u32 alignment_start;
   878:     u32 alignment_count;
   879: };
   880: 
   881: // Integer constant-expression facts retained before enum compatible-type
   882: // selection. `magnitude_high:magnitude` is an unsigned 128-bit mathematical
   883: // magnitude; `is_negative` supplies its sign independently of the expression's
   884: // C type. `type`, `rank`, `bit_width`, and `is_signed` describe the resolved
   885: // semantic integer type after literal typing, casts, promotions, and the usual
   886: // arithmetic conversions. Explicit initializers retain their original ICE
   887: // type; implicit enumerators retain the value and type of their successor step.
   888: typedef enum CIntegerRank
   889: {
   890:     C_INTEGER_RANK_INVALID,
   891:     C_INTEGER_RANK_BOOL,
   892:     C_INTEGER_RANK_CHAR,
   893:     C_INTEGER_RANK_SHORT,
   894:     C_INTEGER_RANK_INT,
   895:     C_INTEGER_RANK_LONG,
   896:     C_INTEGER_RANK_LONG_LONG,
   897:     C_INTEGER_RANK_INT128,
   898:     C_INTEGER_RANK_COUNT,
   899: } CIntegerRank;
   900: 
   901: typedef struct CIntegerConstant CIntegerConstant;
   902: struct CIntegerConstant
   903: {
   904:     u64 magnitude;
   905:     u64 magnitude_high;
   906:     CTypeId type;
   907:     CIntegerRank rank;

   967: struct CEntity

   969:     String8 name;
   970:     // Interned id of `name` (0 when the parse ran without a symbol table);
   971:     // the scope-lookup buckets and chains key on it.
   972:     u32 symbol;
   973:     CSourceLocation location;
   974:     CTypeId type;
   975:     CScopeId scope;
   976:     CEntityId next_in_scope;
   977:     CEntityId next_in_lookup;
   978:     CEntityId next_typedef_in_lookup;
   979:     CEntityId next_by_name;
   980:     u32 declaration_index;
   981:     u32 declaration_token_plus_one;
   982:     // First token of the complete block-scope declaration. Local declarator
   983:     // ranges below deliberately exclude shared specifiers.
   984:     u32 declaration_statement_start;
   985:     u32 declaration_token_start;
   986:     u32 declaration_token_count;
   987:     u32 alignment_start;
   988:     u32 alignment_count;
   989:     CEntityKind kind;
   990:     bool is_definition;
   991:     bool is_static_storage;
   992:     bool is_thread_local;
   993:     bool is_constexpr;
   994:     bool has_constant_value;
   995:     bool constant_is_negative;
   996:     bool has_cleanup;
   997:     bool cleanup_attribute_checked;
   998:     // Register storage on a local. Only the local declarator path records it,
   999:     // and only one reader needs it: an assembler label on a `register` local
  1000:     // binds a machine register instead of renaming a symbol.
  1001:     bool is_register;
  1002:     // Block-scope extern declarations are C_ENTITY_LOCAL for lexical lookup,
  1003:     // but they name external storage rather than an automatic local place.
  1004:     bool is_extern;
  1005:     // The function's only definition so far is GNU inline-only, so the unit
  1006:     // may still give its external definition.
  1007:     bool definition_is_gnu_inline_only;
  1008:     CEntityId cleanup_function;
  1009:     u32 cleanup_attribute_token;
  1010:     u32 cleanup_attribute_end;
  1011:     // One-based member index for enumerators, zero otherwise. Wide enum values
  1012:     // stay in their existing sparse member records, not in every entity.
  1013:     u32 enum_member_plus_one;
  1014:     u64 constant_value;
  1015: };

   694:     // beside them. Each is laid out as two contiguous elements of its

   684:     // storage-only alias: it has its own two-byte layout, its own rank below
   685:     // `float` in the usual arithmetic conversions, and its own place in the
   686:     // vector element ladder.  Clang's `avx512fp16intrin.h` declares
   687:     // `__m512h` out of it, which is what brought it in.
   688:     C_TYPE_FLOAT16,
   689:     C_TYPE_BFLOAT16,
   690:     C_TYPE_FLOAT,
   691:     C_TYPE_DOUBLE,
   692:     C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE,
   693:     // The three C99 complex types and the `_Float16 _Complex` extension
   694:     // beside them. Each is laid out as two contiguous elements of its
   695:     // underlying real type -- real part first -- which is both what the
   696:     // psABIs specify and what lets the IR model them as two-field
   697:     // aggregates (see c_ir_scalar_type). Clang's `_mm512_set1_pch` takes a
   698:     // `_Float16 _Complex` parameter, which is what brought the half form in.
   699:     C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX,
   700:     C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX,
   701:     C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   702:     C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   703:     C_TYPE_VA_LIST,
   704:     C_TYPE_NULLPTR,
   705:     C_TYPE_POINTER,
   706:     C_TYPE_ARRAY,
   707:     C_TYPE_VECTOR,
   708:     C_TYPE_FUNCTION,
   709:     C_TYPE_STRUCT,
   710:     C_TYPE_UNION,
   711:     C_TYPE_ENUM,
   712:     C_TYPE_COUNT,
   713: } CTypeKind;
   714: 
   715: // The complex kinds and their underlying real kind, in both directions. Every
   716: // complex question in the frontend goes through these three so the mapping
   717: // lives in one place; `c_type_kind_complex_of` returns C_TYPE_INVALID for a
   718: // real kind that has no complex counterpart (C only defines the three).
   719: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE bool c_type_kind_is_complex(CTypeKind kind)
   720: {
   721:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX;
   722: }
   723: 
   724: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE CTypeKind c_type_kind_complex_element(CTypeKind kind)
@@ 655:725 @@

   721:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX;
   722: }
   723: 
   724: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE CTypeKind c_type_kind_complex_element(CTypeKind kind)
   725: {
   726:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT16
   727:            : kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT
   728:            : kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX      ? C_TYPE_DOUBLE
   729:            : kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX ? C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE
   730:                                                 : C_TYPE_INVALID;
   731: }
   732:

   734: {
   735:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX
   736:            : kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX
