   667:     C_TYPE_INVALID,
   668:     C_TYPE_VOID,
   669:     C_TYPE_BOOL,
   670:     C_TYPE_CHAR,
   671:     C_TYPE_SIGNED_CHAR,
   672:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_CHAR,
   673:     C_TYPE_SHORT,
   674:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_SHORT,
   675:     C_TYPE_INT,
   676:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_INT,
   677:     C_TYPE_LONG,
   678:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_LONG,
   679:     C_TYPE_LONG_LONG,
   680:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG,
   681:     C_TYPE_INT128,
   682:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_INT128,
   683:     // IEEE-754 binary16, spelled `_Float16`.  It is a real type, not a
   684:     // storage-only alias: it has its own two-byte layout, its own rank below
   685:     // `float` in the usual arithmetic conversions, and its own place in the
   686:     // vector element ladder.  Clang's `avx512fp16intrin.h` declares
   687:     // `__m512h` out of it, which is what brought it in.
   688:     C_TYPE_FLOAT16,
   689:     C_TYPE_BFLOAT16,
   690:     C_TYPE_FLOAT,
   691:     C_TYPE_DOUBLE,
   692:     C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE,
   693:     // The three C99 complex types and the `_Float16 _Complex` extension
   694:     // beside them. Each is laid out as two contiguous elements of its
   695:     // underlying real type -- real part first -- which is both what the
   696:     // psABIs specify and what lets the IR model them as two-field
   697:     // aggregates (see c_ir_scalar_type). Clang's `_mm512_set1_pch` takes a
   698:     // `_Float16 _Complex` parameter, which is what brought the half form in.
   699:     C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX,
   700:     C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX,
   701:     C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   702:     C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   703:     C_TYPE_VA_LIST,
   704:     C_TYPE_NULLPTR,
   705:     C_TYPE_POINTER,
   706:     C_TYPE_ARRAY,
   707:     C_TYPE_VECTOR,
   708:     C_TYPE_FUNCTION,
   709:     C_TYPE_STRUCT,
   710:     C_TYPE_UNION,
   711:     C_TYPE_ENUM,
   712:     C_TYPE_COUNT,
   713: } CTypeKind;
   714: 
   715: // The complex kinds and their underlying real kind, in both directions. Every
   716: // complex question in the frontend goes through these three so the mapping
   717: // lives in one place; `c_type_kind_complex_of` returns C_TYPE_INVALID for a
   718: // real kind that has no complex counterpart (C only defines the three).
   719: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE bool c_type_kind_is_complex(CTypeKind kind)
   720: {
   721:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX;
   722: }
   723: 
   724: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE CTypeKind c_type_kind_complex_element(CTypeKind kind)

   726:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT16

   728:            : kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX      ? C_TYPE_DOUBLE
   729:            : kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX ? C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE
   730:                                                 : C_TYPE_INVALID;
   731: }

   733: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE CTypeKind c_type_kind_complex_of(CTypeKind kind)
   734: {
   735:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX
   736:            : kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX
   737:            : kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE      ? C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX
   738:            : kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE ? C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX

   673:     C_TYPE_SHORT,
   674:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_SHORT,
   675:     C_TYPE_INT,
   676:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_INT,
   677:     C_TYPE_LONG,
   678:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_LONG,
   679:     C_TYPE_LONG_LONG,
   680:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_LONG_LONG,
   681:     C_TYPE_INT128,
   682:     C_TYPE_UNSIGNED_INT128,
   683:     // IEEE-754 binary16, spelled `_Float16`.  It is a real type, not a
   684:     // storage-only alias: it has its own two-byte layout, its own rank below
   685:     // `float` in the usual arithmetic conversions, and its own place in the
   686:     // vector element ladder.  Clang's `avx512fp16intrin.h` declares
   687:     // `__m512h` out of it, which is what brought it in.
   688:     C_TYPE_FLOAT16,
   689:     C_TYPE_BFLOAT16,
   690:     C_TYPE_FLOAT,
   691:     C_TYPE_DOUBLE,
   692:     C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE,
   693:     // The three C99 complex types and the `_Float16 _Complex` extension
   694:     // beside them. Each is laid out as two contiguous elements of its
   695:     // underlying real type -- real part first -- which is both what the
   696:     // psABIs specify and what lets the IR model them as two-field
   697:     // aggregates (see c_ir_scalar_type). Clang's `_mm512_set1_pch` takes a
   698:     // `_Float16 _Complex` parameter, which is what brought the half form in.
   699:     C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX,
   700:     C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX,
   701:     C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   702:     C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   703:     C_TYPE_VA_LIST,
   704:     C_TYPE_NULLPTR,
   705:     C_TYPE_POINTER,
   706:     C_TYPE_ARRAY,
   707:     C_TYPE_VECTOR,
   708:     C_TYPE_FUNCTION,
   709:     C_TYPE_STRUCT,
   710:     C_TYPE_UNION,
   711:     C_TYPE_ENUM,
   712:     C_TYPE_COUNT,
   713: } CTypeKind;
   714: 
   715: // The complex kinds and their underlying real kind, in both directions. Every
   716: // complex question in the frontend goes through these three so the mapping
   717: // lives in one place; `c_type_kind_complex_of` returns C_TYPE_INVALID for a
   718: // real kind that has no complex counterpart (C only defines the three).
   719: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE bool c_type_kind_is_complex(CTypeKind kind)
   720: {
   721:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX;
   722: }
   723: 
   724: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE CTypeKind c_type_kind_complex_element(CTypeKind kind)
   725: {
   726:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT16
   727:            : kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT
   728:            : kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX      ? C_TYPE_DOUBLE
   729:            : kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX ? C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE
   730:                                                 : C_TYPE_INVALID;
   731: }
   732: 
   733: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE CTypeKind c_type_kind_complex_of(CTypeKind kind)

   735:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX
   736:            : kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX
   737:            : kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE      ? C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX

   689:     C_TYPE_BFLOAT16,
   690:     C_TYPE_FLOAT,
   691:     C_TYPE_DOUBLE,
   692:     C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE,
   693:     // The three C99 complex types and the `_Float16 _Complex` extension
   694:     // beside them. Each is laid out as two contiguous elements of its
   695:     // underlying real type -- real part first -- which is both what the
   696:     // psABIs specify and what lets the IR model them as two-field
   697:     // aggregates (see c_ir_scalar_type). Clang's `_mm512_set1_pch` takes a
   698:     // `_Float16 _Complex` parameter, which is what brought the half form in.
   699:     C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX,
   700:     C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX,
   701:     C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   702:     C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX,
   703:     C_TYPE_VA_LIST,
   704:     C_TYPE_NULLPTR,
   705:     C_TYPE_POINTER,
   706:     C_TYPE_ARRAY,
   707:     C_TYPE_VECTOR,
   708:     C_TYPE_FUNCTION,
   709:     C_TYPE_STRUCT,
   710:     C_TYPE_UNION,
   711:     C_TYPE_ENUM,
   712:     C_TYPE_COUNT,
   713: } CTypeKind;
   714: 
   715: // The complex kinds and their underlying real kind, in both directions. Every
   716: // complex question in the frontend goes through these three so the mapping
   717: // lives in one place; `c_type_kind_complex_of` returns C_TYPE_INVALID for a
   718: // real kind that has no complex counterpart (C only defines the three).
   719: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE bool c_type_kind_is_complex(CTypeKind kind)
   720: {
   721:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX || kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX;
   722: }
   723: 
   724: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE CTypeKind c_type_kind_complex_element(CTypeKind kind)
   725: {
   726:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT16
   727:            : kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT
   728:            : kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX      ? C_TYPE_DOUBLE
   729:            : kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX ? C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE
   730:                                                 : C_TYPE_INVALID;
   731: }
   732: 
   733: BUSTER_GLOBAL_LOCAL BUSTER_UNUSED_DECL BUSTER_INLINE CTypeKind c_type_kind_complex_of(CTypeKind kind)
   734: {
   735:     return kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT16       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT16_COMPLEX
   736:            : kind == C_TYPE_FLOAT       ? C_TYPE_FLOAT_COMPLEX
   737:            : kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE      ? C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX
   738:            : kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE ? C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX
   739:                                         : C_TYPE_INVALID;
   740: }
   741: 
   742: typedef struct CArrayBound CArrayBound;
   743: struct CArrayBound
   744: {
   745:     u64 inferred_count;
   746:     u32 token_start;
   747:     u32 token_count;
   748:     bool is_static;
   749:     bool is_star;
   750:     bool has_inferred_count;
   751:     // A `const` written inside the brackets. A parameter declared with it is
   752:     // adjusted to a const pointer (C17 6.7.6.3p7), so `int a[const 2]` is
   753:     // not modifiable although its elements are.
   754:     bool is_const;
   755: };

   763:     // xmmintrin.h defines `struct __mm_storeh_pi_struct` inside two
   764:     // intrinsics -- and a reference resolves to the innermost visible one.
   765:     // Meaningful only for struct/union/enum types with a tag; everything
   766:     // else leaves it zero, which is the file scope.

   718: // real kind that has no complex counterpart (C only defines the three).

   728:            : kind == C_TYPE_DOUBLE_COMPLEX      ? C_TYPE_DOUBLE
   729:            : kind == C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE_COMPLEX ? C_TYPE_LONG_DOUBLE

===== COMMA EXPRESSION TEST OCCURRENCES =====
--- tests/basic_c_typeof_comma_base.c:1 ---
     1: // `__typeof__` over a comma expression's subscript -- Py_XSETREF through
     2: // _PyTuple_ITEMS expands to `((void)0, (cast)->ob_item)[0]` -- resolves
     3: // through the last comma operand, and the base's own parentheses only strip
     4: // when they wrap the whole range, or `(cast)(operand)` peels into soup.
     5: typedef struct tuple { long refs; void* ob_item[4]; } PyTupleObject;
     6: void set_slot(void* args, void* result)
     7: {
     8:     __typeof__(((void)0, (((void)(0), ((PyTupleObject*)(args)))->ob_item))[0])* _tmp_dst_ptr =
     9:         &(((void)0, (((void)(0), ((PyTupleObject*)(args)))->ob_item))[0]);
    10:     *_tmp_dst_ptr = result;
    11: }
